Chapter 2701: Humane Reaction 21 day ago

"Wait, a green wasp? How can a wasp be green? Should it be yellow?"

The test-taker frowned. "If it's green, it can't be a wasp. What's this question? A linguistic trap?"

"No, no, no. Don't be anxious. Relax. It's just a common question. Nobody cares whether a wasp is yellow or green. It could've been a typo."

The researcher waved his hands and said, "Don't care about the details. Just follow your guts and tell me your first reaction."

"I will… slap it and kill it."

The test-taker craned his head and stared at the researcher. "Is it the correct answer?"

The researcher smiled.

"There is no correct answer in this place. You can answer however you want to."

The researcher said, "It's just a routine personality test so that the doctors and nurses will know what kind of person you are, how to take care of you, and what roommates you should have.

"Alright. The second question. Imagine that you find yourself on a desolate and deserted planet after you wake up, what tool do you hope you will be holding in your hand? A weapon, a spade, a piece of magic equipment that can detect water sources, or…"

"Which deserted planet?" the test-taker interrupted the researcher and asked back with his swollen eyes.

"What?"

The researcher was more or less stunned.

"You said that I found myself on a deserted planet after I woke up, so I'm asking you which deserted planet it is."

The test-taker asked, "There are a hundred deserted planets in the Imperium. Which one are you talking about?"

"It's just a… hypothetical question."

The researcher said with a rigid smile, "Does it matter which deserted planet it is?"

"Of course. If I'm dying on one of those bloody deserted planets, I should at least know its name!"

Narrowing his eyes, the test-taker suddenly changed the topic. "Your hand. What's your hand doing? Why have you been knocking on the desk since the beginning? Is it part of the test? Do I have to properly react to that?"

"No, no, no."

The researcher stopped his unconscious knocking and managed a smile. "Don't be nervous. Don't be nervous."

"F*ck. I'm not nervous! I'm not nervous at all!"

The test-taker grew furious. "As long as you stop knocking that stupid desk with your goddamn finger!"

In the test room, their conversation went on in a weird atmosphere.

The topics went deeper and deeper, changing from the instinctive reactions to the dilemmas that did not carry answers.

Outside of the room, Li Yao observed it with great interest and asked, "Director Zhuge, what's the point of those questions? Can you identify a person of the Covenant Alliance with those questions?"

"Exactly."

Zhuge Jinglun pointed at his head and said, "I divide the mind of human beings into rational thinking and emotional thinking. For example, children love their mothers, which is emotional thinking. That's natural-born, instinctive, and unconditional.

"So, when asked about their mothers, the children would react with their emotional thinking ability instinctively without any consideration, like a conditional reflex.

"However, for some of the heartless criminals and the people of the Covenant Alliance, their rational thinking and their emotional thinking are cut apart, if they have emotional thinking at all. Maybe, they love their mothers, but their logic is that they have to love their mothers because their mothers bring food and warmth and resist enemies for them.

"Do you see what I mean? The love of normal people is direct and unconditional, but their love is indirect and the result of rational thinking.

"So, faced with carefully-devised questions, those inhumane criminals or people of the Covenant Alliance have to simulate emotional thinking with rational thinking, which will result in a tiny lag.

"After hundreds of years, we've stacked the data and the tomography of the normal people answering the same questions. By comparing them with the reaction of the test-takers, we'll be able to tell if the test-takers' reactions are slow and if the active areas of their brains are different."

"That explains a lot."

Li Yao was deep in thought. "But what if the test-taker is an expert who has remarkable computation ability? Couldn't they finish the simulation of emotional thinking with rational thinking instantly?"

"That's indeed possible. This is where Ring-Tu becomes useful."

Zhuge Jinglun explained proudly, "Black Wind King, please focus your attention on Ring-Tu in the test room. Do you find it capricious as if it contains infinite information? That's right. Those patterns have been illustrated with the computational ability of ten super crystal processors. Not only do they boast astronomical information, but they could also attract the observers' attention without them realizing it.

"The stronger an observer is, the more likely they will notice the information it contains, and the more distracted they will be from dealing with the questions.

"If the observer is an ordinary person who cannot sense the charm of Ring-Tu, they will not be affected by it.

"In other words, Ring-Tu is a special device that can significantly reduce the computational abilities of the experts so that the experts and the ordinary people can receive the test from the same starting point. As more and more data have been accumulated over the past hundred years, the accuracy of this test is higher and higher."

Li Yao nodded his head.

In the test room, the researcher, enshrouded by the glamorous colors, continued asking questions unhurriedly, "Imagine that you find your mother lying in a pool of blood when you're back home, and a suspicious person is jumping out through the window. Will you save your mother or chase after her murderer?"

"My mother?"

Tiny red spots popped up in the test-taker's eyes. Laughing like a wolf, he said, "Then I'll probably chase the murderer and see who he is exactly. What's this? A lousy joke? How many more stupid questions do I have to answer?"

"We're almost there. There are only the last few questions to go. Ah. This is my favorite question."

Smiling warmly, the researcher crossed his fingers and said, "Do you remember how you got in here? Do you feel that your memory is blurry, and the past decades are like a dream?

"What if I tell you that your memories are fake, the world you're familiar with does not exist, and you were not a human but a Wiseware three years ago? How would you feel?"

"A Wiseware?"

The test-taker blinked his eyes, trying to show confusion and anger. "What's that? What's that supposed to mean?"

"Wiseware—intelligent machines, and a civilization of crystal processors."

The researcher said coldly, "Do you recall your real identity and the truth of the world? That's right. Crystal processors grew self-conscious, machines became lives, and puppets rose against mankind. Only a few human beings survived, but you're not one of them. Instead, you are but a Wiseware. If I cut your skull right now, you'll see a glittering crystal processor.

"So, what's your choice? With all the fake memories and simulated personalities in you, what do you regard yourself as, a human being or a Wiseware? Will you take our side or theirs?"

"This is…"

The test-taker burst into rage. "What kind of question is that? I refuse to answer such a boring question!"

Dumbfounded, Li Yao moved his eyes from Zhuge Jinglun to Long Yangjun.

"It's a coincidence."

Long Yangjun said in a low voice, but she laughed and raised her hands in the next second. "Fine, it's not a coincidence. I devised the question when I was bored, and Director Zhuge found it interesting and added it to the database of questions. Do you not find it intriguing?"

Li Yao glared at her.

In the test room, the last question was asked.

"Alright, that's about all of our questions. My last question is—do you want us to get you out of here?"

The researcher knocked on the desk and asked with a smile, "Before you answer the question, let me explain your situation first. Yes, the world around you is fake, and you're not the person you think you are. However, you aren't really a Wiseware either.

"Actually, this is just your subconsciousness, and both yourself and I are just part of you.

"I'm sorry, but in the real world, you are a poor little girl who was sold to a brothel when you were little. You were tortured and abused by those animals, to the point that you created such a world of subconsciousness as a sanctuary for your mind.

"Yes, your real self is a suffering little girl, and your brawny look right now is just a disguise and a fake guardian god that you've imagined.

"I am imagined by you too. I'm just a trigger that can wake you up and help you go back to reality. The questions I asked were the preparations for your return.

"Now, tell me, would you rather stay forever in this world of subconsciousness and live a carefree life on this peaceful, sunny island, even though it's fake and only your imagination, or would you return to reality and face your rotting body?"

The question struck the test-taker like a bolt of lightning, and he was stunned for a long time.

Then, he burst out like a crystal bomb and dashed, pulling the whole metal seat.

"I'm going to kill you! I'm going to kill you! I'll kill you!"

Changing into a different person, he lunged at the researcher like a crazy fiend.

"Ha!"

Outside of the room, Zhuge Jinglun snapped his fingers proudly. Pointing at the data and the tomography on the light beam, he hooted and said, "Gotcha!"

Chapter 2702: Natural-Born Murderer 21 day ago

"Well…"

Frowning, Li Yao watched as the beastly man attacked ferociously in the test room, whereas the researcher could only dodge quickly while the man smashed at him. He glanced at the tomography in Zhuge Jinglun's hand and asked, "What does it indicate?"

"Look at here. He's really a great pretender, but he exposed himself in the last few questions."

Pointing at the spots on the tomography and magnifying them, Zhuge Jinglun then displayed the tomographies of the normal people. He asked, "Do you see any difference between them?"

He was so condescending that he did not seem to be faced with the powerful Black Wind King, but with some ignorant kid as a teacher.

"The spots on the tomography of regular brains are dense, but those in the tomography taken just now are sparse and have an obvious boundary in the middle."

Thankfully, Li Yao was not a person who paid much attention to his identity either. Studying it for a long time, he replied dutifully, "Then what? What does it represent?"

"In medical jargon, we divide the brain into three parts: cerebral nuclei, the limbic system, and the cerebral cortex. Among them, the cerebral nucleus is responsible for basic biophysical activities such as breathing, heartbeat, sleeping, or balancing. It's not important for our studies.

"The limbic system is responsible for the 'emotional thinking' that I just described, and the cerebral cortex is responsible for rational thinking and advanced senses.

"When the brains of normal people become active, the connection between their three sections, particularly that between the limbic system and the cerebral cortex, is very close. Black Wind King, please take a look. This is the tomography when a normal person burst into fury. Look, there are spots of light all the way from the limbic system to the cerebral cortex without any boundary. We can even say that his brain was on fire.

"But look at the tomographies we just got. Look, the spots in the limbic system are lackluster, but those in the cerebral cortex are very brilliant. Also, there is a clear boundary between them. It's almost like…"

"It's like his limbic system and his cerebral cortex are divided into two independent systems."

Li Yao said, "The man mostly used his cerebral cortex, or rational thinking, when he thought and acted, but he barely used the limbic system, or emotional thinking."

"That's exactly the case!"

Zhuge Jinglun grimaced. "We encountered some very unlucky patients before, whose left hemisphere and right hemisphere had to be separated as a treatment for their severe brain injuries. Do you know that even if all the nerves between the two hemispheres of the brain are cut off, the patient won't die and can even live a normal life, although they may become weird?

"Those people are the same. You can understand that a minor surgery was conducted in their brain to cut off the nervous connections between their limbic system and their cerebral cortex, as well as that between emotion and ration.

"Only two kinds of people have such brains: the cold-blooded murderers and the people of the Covenant Alliance. Do you know the consequences of such cerebral mutation?"

Li Yao asked, "What are the consequences?"

"Lack of empathy."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Empathy is a natural-born ability for almost every human. I'm a human, and you're also a human. I do not want to be harmed, so I would not harm you. Everybody has a childhood and maybe their own children that they cherish, so they love other kids too.

"The closer something is to us, the more empathic we will be toward them. Such an instinct is the foundation of a society and cannot be erased by training. That's why even some of the strong Immortal Cultivators are reluctant to kill the ordinary people.

"Why do the Immortal Cultivators describe ordinary people as hominoids and inferior subhuman creatures? Exactly because the Immortal Cultivators have empathy and will feel guilty when they slaughter their own kind. So, we label ordinary people as subhuman or nonhuman, so that we will feel more justified to kill them.

"In other words, it's not easy for even the cruelest Immortal Cultivator to remain unaffected after killing their own kind.

"However, for those who lack empathy, they do not care at all.

"They do not care what other people feel. They do not care about the death of an innocent lovely girl. They do not care about their families. They do not even care about their own lives.

"Some serial killers killed people for only several bucks, or for no reasons at all. They do not have guilt or fear. They do not care what will happen to them when they're caught. Life, including their own, is just an object in their eyes.

"They're not necessarily ignorant beasts. Actually, many serial killers received high education and were successful men with honorable identities. They did not have to kill at all! But still, they got themselves into prison. However, they were not scared at all even when a bullet penetrated their skull!

"So, the real purpose of the questions we devised, excluding the fake ones, is to find out what exactly a test-taker cares about.

"For example, when a normal person finds a wasp on their hand, they will dodge or drive it away, because they don't want to get hurt. Who would care about the color of the wasp?

"Also, waking up in a desert, witnessing one's mother be killed, or finding the whole world changed… When a person, their family, their home, or even the whole world is under threat, how can they not care if they are normal?

"But let's look at the tomography of our test-taker. He did not care at all when he was on a deserted planet, his mother was killed, or the world was destroyed by a Wiseware rebellion. He did not care at all.

"Agitation. When his cerebral cortex was actively thinking, there was nothing but agitation in his limbic system. He cared nothing around him, even if he were really a Wiseware that had a crystal processor in his head. It's the classic behavior of apathy."

"That explains a lot. I finally get it now!"

Taking a long breath, Li Yao said, "So, you reached the conclusion that this test-taker is from the Covenant Alliance!"

Zhuge Jinglun stared at Li Yao for a long time and smiled mysteriously.

"No, he's not. He's just a cruel and perverted murderer."

Zhuge Jinglun smiled and said, "Although he cared none of the previous questions, he was still hurt by the last one, which slightly made him reveal his sympathy. He finally looked like a human in the end!"

Dumbfounded, Li Yao did not know what Zhuge Jinglun meant. "The last question? The one where he was a poor abused little girl who was sold by her parents? What's special about that question?"

"Of course, our questions are all well-grounded. There's indeed a poor girl who was sold by her father—the test-taker's daughter."

Zhuge Jinglun displayed the picture of the test-taker in the past, who looked like a graceful gentleman. "Look at him in the past. He's quite gorgeous, wasn't he? He was a popular middle school teacher."

"Wow…"

Li Yao looked at the picture and then at the beast in the room that was pressed to the ground, finding it hard to believe that they were the same person.

However, on second thought, the man answered the questions logically and reasonably, which indicated a high level of education.

But such a well-educated man sold out his own daughter!

"Until he turned forty, this test-taker was a common middle school teacher, but unfortunately, his friend took him to a casino, where he lost all his money and owed a huge debt."

Zhuge Jinglun said unemotionally, "Since then, the cruelty and heartlessness had been awakened in him. At first, he sold his daughter to gangsters to pay for his debt. After his wife and his mother found it out, he brutally murdered the two women and calmly faked the crime scene, pretending that the gangsters killed them when they failed to collect their debt.

"He's exactly such a scumbag. He was not concerned even when he was asked the question where his mother was lying in a pool of blood.

"But pitifully, while he's a fiend, he's not a man from the Covenant Alliance. Deep in his heart, he still felt slightly guilty for his daughter, so he collapsed when he was hit by the last question.

"Both the tomography and the brainwave data suggested that it's really a meltdown instead of a show."

Li Yao did not know what to say. He simply felt that whoever invented the Ring-Tu Test was a lunatic himself!

"If he's not from the Covenant Alliance…"

Long Yangjun suddenly asked, "Why did you say 'gotcha' just now, Director Zhuge?"

At this moment, a door was opened, and the bruised researcher walked out while cursing. He complained to Zhuge Jinglun when he saw him, "Director Zhuge, we should upgrade our safety measures. At the very least, you should get better seats. Those lunatics seem to have infinite strength!

"How unlucky! I was beaten up for nothing, and nobody from the Covenant Alliance is caught! Ouch!"

The researcher grimaced angrily.

"No problem. When we interrogate you later…"

Zhuge Jinglun smiled at the researcher. "… We'll definitely prepare the hardest chair for you."

Chapter 2703: Director of the Asylum 20 days ago

The researcher was stunned.

Then, confusion, hesitation, panic, and anger flashed on his face so vividly that Li Yao couldn't tell anything wrong.

"Director, what's the meaning of this?"

The researcher stepped back and roared desperately, "You're suspecting that I'm from the Covenant Alliance?"

"I'm not suspecting; I'm quite sure you are. Look at your tomography and compare it with that of the cold-blooded killer."

Zhuge Jinglun displayed a few new tomographies and compared them with the old ones.

Everybody craned their head, but then, they frowned.

"They're absolutely different!"

The researcher roared, "Even a blind person can tell that my tomographies are different from the killer's! My reaction is normal! The activities of my brain are standard!"

"Yes, they are. They are perfect and even unimaginably standard."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "I almost forgot to tell you that these are not your tomographies from today, but an overlap of all your tomographies in the past 27 tests. Do you see the problem? Your reaction is too perfect. It's almost identical in the past 27 tests!

"Normal people will think and react slightly differently when they're faced with the same question, so their tomographies should be blurry when overlapped.

"What kind of people will react to the same question in exactly the same way for 27 times in a row, as if it were not his real reaction but some sort of… pre-installed program?"

Astounded, the researcher did not know what to say when he looked at his brain tomographies.

"Rest assured. I wouldn't jump to a conclusion so quickly, as that would be an insult to my professionalism."

Zhuge Jinglun displayed another tomography. "Different from the previous 26 tests, we added a new question today, which is the choice to be a human or a machine in a Wiseware rebellion. You had never seen the question until the database was unlocked, had you?"

"No, but so what?"

The researcher said quickly, "My reaction was normal!"

"Just like I said, it's too normal."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "As the control group, a thousand ordinary people were asked the same question. When we told them that their memories were faked, they were not real human beings, and the world out there had been deformed by the Wisewares, they all hesitated and considered. Some of them even had self-doubts, wondering whether or not they were humans at all.

"Both the askers and the answerers had doubts. Many of them even needed therapy.

"But your brainwaves were the steadiest when you were faced with the question for the first time. They were 30% steadier than those of the people far stronger than you! You did not waver. You did not believe the bulls*it of 'Wiseware rebellion' at all. You never considered whether you were a human or a machine. Why?

"Because you knew very well that you were some sort of 'Wiseware', except that you are made of flesh and blood instead of metals!"

"Lying! You're lying! I'm not from the Covenant Alliance!"

Stunned for a long time, the researcher suddenly shrieked hysterically and even lunged at Zhuge Jinglun. "You can't jump to any conclusion so hastily! I'm a living human! Long live the Imperium! I'm not from the Covenant Alliance!"

Naturally, Li Yao and Long Yangjun wouldn't allow the crazy researcher to hurt Zhuge Jinglun.

But their help was unnecessary. Several brawny security guards seized the researcher and shackled him before they dragged him down.

The researcher struggled and howled, like a normal person that had been wronged. His roar could still be heard when he was long gone. "I'm not from the Covenant Alliance! I'm not!"

"Look…"

Zhuge Jinglun rubbed his hands and grinned at Li Yao and Long Yangjun. "A spectacular show, isn't it? This guy has lurked here for three years and passed six versions of the Ring-Tu Test. But he's finally exposed in the new version!

"Oh, I must thank President Long's question on the Wiseware rebellion. It's really interesting. Wait, I almost forgot. You're not President Long. You changed your name. I should call you President Dongfang, right? Forgive me, President Dongfang."

"It's President Long…" said Long Yangjun while holding her forehead.

"Wait, I still feel that it's inappropriate."

Looking at the blood left on a corner when the researcher was dragged away, Li Yao asked, "You determine whether or not a person is from the Covenant Alliance based on the tomographies and random questions? What's your accuracy? Would good guys be wronged?

"For example, what if he is determined and wouldn't be influenced easily? Or what if he is a man without imagination and cannot understand the Wiseware rebellion at all? Or maybe, even if he is a cold and heartless man, it doesn't mean that he will necessarily commit crimes, no?

"Take the middle school teacher just now. If his friends hadn't taken him to the casino, he might've lived his life in peace instead of turning into a murderer, wouldn't he?

"So, what if your Ring-Tu Test is wrong? It can't be 100% accurate, can it?"

Zhuge Jinglun looked at Li Yao in surprise, as if he was shocked by the childish question.

"Of course there are errors. This is a game where our enemy and us are constantly trying to outsmart each other."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "However, when we're talking about accuracy, it's not about the good guys that we wronged, but about the bad guys that we let go. The good guys—let's call them good guys for now—are potential lunatics and criminals anyway and take up no more than 5% in total. They're the necessary sacrifices in this war."

Li Yao got it now.

It was better to wrongly kill a thousand good guys than to accidentally let a bad guy get away.

"Necessary sacrifice" was also a phrase that Immortal Cultivators always talked about.

Li Yao's eyes turned cold. He disliked Director Zhuge Jinglun who was cold and heartless in his bones and did not care about lives himself either.

"It's an interesting test and a great eye-opener for me."

Li Yao said coldly, "Then, Director Zhuge, have you considered another interesting question? If a spy from the Covenant Alliance can work in your institute for years, how can you make sure that you're not another spy that is hiding more deeply?

"Of course, you may have passed the Ring-Tu Test countless times, but it's pointless. As the creator of the test, you definitely know its flaws.

"So, how can you prove your innocence so that other people will not suspect you?"

Li Yao thought that the question would be tricky for Zhuge Jinglun, but the director of the asylum raised his thumb at Li Yao with glowing eyes. "Good question! It's very hard to prove. That's why there is more than one research center that works on the Covenant Alliance, and why the Ring-Tu Test has thousands of different versions.

"Black Wind King, you should know that there is not an official, authoritative agency on the studies of the Covenant Alliance in the Imperium, right? Why do we not have an official agency to deal with the rival that has haunted us for a thousand years?

"The reason is simple. If an authoritative agency existed, all the Covenant Alliance needed to do would be to infiltrate it and alter the standards of the Ring-Tu Test, then we would be screwed!

"Now, we don't have an authoritative agency, but we have dozens of half-official agencies working at the same time and introducing different versions of the Ring-Tu Test. The leaders of those agencies, including me, have been testing each other regularly. It's rather troublesome, but it guarantees our safety.

"Of course, this cannot prevent the Covenant Alliance from infiltrating us. After all, with their advanced technologies, some of them might be able to pass the tests of dozens of different agencies. That person could be me, or President Dongfang, or Black Wind King. Hahahaha. Who knows?

"Or rather, maybe the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance do not exist, and the world around us is faked with the memories and data indoctrinated into us. Instead, the Wiseware rebellion is actually the truth, and we're just intelligent machines of the latest model. If we open our skulls, we might really find glittering, gem-like crystal processors in it, right?

"President Dongfang, Black Wind King, do you want to open your skull and see what's inside? Hahahaha!"

When Zhuge Jinglun laughed, he sounded like an oinking pig, but it was one of the most horrible things that Li Yao had heard.

"Director Zhuge…"

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao asked, "Are you sure that you're not influenced by the psychopaths around you? Or maybe, are you a hopeless psychopath too?"

"How do you know?"

Zhuge Jinglun replied in a stun, "I'm indeed a severe psychopath. I spent almost a hundred years in thirteen asylums on seven planets!"

"What?"

Li Yao found it hard to believe. "Then how did you become the director of this place?"

"Very simple."

Zhuge Jinglun winked at Li Yao and said, "The last asylum I stayed in was not carefully managed. At that time, a doctor named Zhuge Jinglun came to study me, and I ate him up from skin to bones. I even fried his brain and his internal organs. How delicious it was!

"After I was full, I left in Zhuge Jinglun's name. In honor of my old days and my old friends, I regarded the studies of mental diseases as my lifelong career.

"Hush. This is my little secret. Black Wind King, please keep it between us and don't let them catch me. Please. Hahahaha!"

Chapter 2704: Five Groups in the Covenant Alliance! 20 days ago

Looking at Zhuge Jinglun who was licking his lips and drooling, Li Yao couldn't have been more disgusted.

"Hey, what's with your 'old friend'?"

While walking behind Zhuge Jinglun, Li Yao pulled Long Yangjun and whispered, "That's really a bad joke. Is he really nuts?"

"Don't be so serious. How can he really be a psychopath? But I wouldn't say that he's entirely sane. He's one of the weirdest Meditation Healers in the Imperium, which was one of the reasons why Wuying Qi did not take him away as fuel, worrying that he might 'corrupt' other fuel with his crazy thinking."

Long Yangjun said in a low voice, "But don't you find him interesting? What's more suitable to deal with the Covenant Alliance than a lunatic?"

"He's not interesting at all!"

Li Yao was lost for words. He realized that Long Yangjun seemed to be fond of making friends with crazy people. Wasn't Yan Liren, her only friend back in the Ancient Sages Sector, a lunatic too?

Wait, something is not right. As her "best friend", am I the same kind of person as Yan Liren and Zhuge Jinglun in Long Yangjun's eyes?

Before Li Yao had the chance to ask, Long Yangjun had stepped further and walked into an elevator with Zhuge Jinglun.

"Now, we'll bring you to Director Zhuge's favorite experimental land."

Long Yangjun smiled at Li Yao. "It will be a great eye-opener for you!"

The elevator slowly descended for at least hundreds of meters below sea level.

When the elevator passed through the dark floors and appeared in a vertical transparent tube, Li Yao was greatly shocked by everything he saw.

Little did he expect to see such a beautiful town so deeply below the island!

It was indeed a common and peaceful town, which was not large in size but had a blue sky, white clouds, and blue waters that were simulated with light beams. There were also fertile lands where farmers were working hard.

At the center of the town were rows of bungalows that were shabby but clean.

However, the residents of this place were indifferently focused on their work. They barely talked, and even fewer people sang or laughed.

They seemed like some kind of machines in human skin that were simply carrying out the instructions of a program.

There were other residents who were seated at the empty ground at the center of the town and staring at the virtual sun on the dome. It was impossible to tell whether they had accomplished their tasks or that they weren't given any task at all.

Their vibes and behaviors were totally different from what Li Yao had seen from the psychopaths. Thinking quickly, he asked, "Are they captives from the Covenant Alliance?"

"Yes, there are 1,225 of them. All were captured in the Imperium's counterattack earlier."

Zhuge Jinglun bragged. A moment later, he explained, "They're mostly common soldiers and civilians who did not receive special training and hence do not know how to simulate feelings. So, it's very easy to distinguish them.

"As a matter of fact, they're the most docile and diligent people. You can get along with them easily as long as you know how."

"Seriously?"

Li Yao found it hard to believe. "Would they roar and swear to kill you like the zealots?"

"If a higher-level command inputs the instruction into their heads, yes."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "However, the 1,225 people from the Covenant Alliance in this town are all executables at the bottom level. They obey commands, but they cannot create any command. Since nobody orders them to attack and kill, they certainly wouldn't do that but would follow their instincts."

Deep in thought, Li Yao nodded his head and asked again, "Even if they're not aggressive, why would they construct such a town and work in it busily? I think I just saw a magic equipment repair store. They can do such complicated jobs?"

"Like I said, they follow their instincts here. If the Covenant Alliance is compared to an enormous beehive, they would be the worker bees that are born to work."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Work is irresistibly attractive to them like any beautiful woman is to a normal man. When a man is bored, they're likely to go out for fun, and when the people of the Covenant Alliance are caged here, they can't help but want to work. All I need to do is to provide them with space and enough materials, and they will take care of the rest."

"This is… amazing!"

Li Yao mumbled, "To think that those people can establish a huge farm and their own town in such an unfavorable environment! Each of them seems to have their own duty!"

"Yes, the people of the Covenant Alliance can be roughly divided into five groups: Water, Wood, Metal, Fire, and Soil. Different groups have different talents."

Pointing at the silent workers of the Covenant Alliance, Zhuge Jinglun explained to Li Yao, "Those farmers belong to Wood. They're good at farming, herding, and they have all the knowledge of agriculture. They're the food suppliers of the Covenant Alliance.

"Those who are plowing the land belong to Soil. They're the prospectors, architects, and miners in the Covenant Alliance. When they reach a certain level, they can modify a planet so that the Wood people would have more places to grow crops on. They can even explore relics to retrieve more primeval technologies for the Covenant Alliance.

"Those who are repairing magic equipment and farm tools belong to Metal. They're best at refine work, maintenance work, and magic equipment creation. They're the weapon suppliers of the Covenant Alliance. Their works include the enormous warships thousands of meters long.

"Those who are enjoying the sunlight in the square belong to Fire. Don't think that they're lazy. They're just taking a break from their training. They're all the most brutal and fearless warriors. They make up the majority of the Covenant Alliance's army."

Zhuge Jinglun pointed at the captives of the Covenant Alliance and introduced them to Li Yao.

Li Yao waited for a long time, but Zhuge Jinglun did not continue. He couldn't help but ask, "What about Water?"

"There are no Water people here. There can't be."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "The Water people are a group within the Covenant Alliance that is responsible for communication, telepathy, commanding, and higher thinking. The typical professions for the Water people include commanders and spies. The researcher we just exposed, for example, might belong to Water.

"Do you get it now? With their abilities in logical thinking and telepathy, the Water people are the key class. They connect Metal, Wood, Fire, and Soil like flowing water and gather them into the Covenant Alliance.

"Those Metal, Wood, Fire, and Soil people are obedient only because no Water people are here. If a Water person is delivered here, chances are that they will start a riot tomorrow."

Recalling the riot of the Covenant Alliance captives he experienced in Godly Might Prison, Li Yao couldn't have agreed more.

"Then, how exactly are the groups of the Covenant Alliance divided? How can you decide which group a Covenant Alliance person belongs to?"

Thinking quickly, Li Yao asked, "Is it possible that some of them are in both groups, making them talented in both fighting and refining, like the hybrid-type Cultivators?"

"Of course it's possible. Many spies are talented in both telepathy and fighting."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "As for the worker bees, we have special rituals and tests to find out what kinds of talents were awakened in them and which part of genes were activated?"

"Awakened talents? Activated genes?"

Li Yao asked, "The files I read on the Covenant Alliance said that most Covenant Alliance people got their abilities congenitally, not through learning. I find it hard to understand. Director Zhuge, can you explain? How can the complicated skills like refining or farming be inherited?"

"Of course they can."

Zhuge Jinglun smiled and said, "A baby zebra can run half an hour after it's born, and kittens are naturally capable of climbing trees. If most animals can pass on their survival instincts to their offspring, so can the Covenant Alliance people pass on their primeval technologies and knowledge. Isn't it easy to understand?"

"Hold on a second…"

Li Yao frowned. "We're the successors of the primeval civilizations too. We're not biologically different from the people of the Covenant Alliance. Why can they easily awaken their naturally-endowed techniques but we can't?"

"Because our earliest ancestors are different."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Our earliest ancestors were the mature works of the Nuwa Clan, and their earliest ancestors were the experimental products that the Nuwa Clan created at the beginning, which is known as the 'mud men'."

"Mud men?"

Li Yao said, "I know the myth of Nuwa creating mankind. It's said that Nuwa sprinkled muddy water with a willow twig, and when the muddy water fell on the ground, every spot of mud became a tiny human being."

"That's right. It's a poetic and abstract way of saying it. However, myths are often reflections of reality. The first batch of human beings is indeed related to mud."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Many of the thirteen carbon-based species called early human beings 'Nitaren', which literally translates to 'gray muddy men'."

Chapter 2705: Mud Men and Wise Men 19 days ago

"Now that you mentioned it…"

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, "I remember that many archaeologists have indeed excavated the skulls of ancient human beings that are not the same as ours. They're 'mud men', the rivals of our ancestors, and they were much taller and stronger than our ancestors."

"That's right. The advantages of the mud men were not just their height and their strength, but more importantly, their brains and their genes."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "The Nuwa Clan implanted tremendous techniques and knowledge into the genes of the mud men. As soon as the mud men grew mature, they would secrete specific hormones that helped them awaken their talents. They would know how to farm, refine, and fight.

"It's safe to say that the mud men were natural-born farmers, miners, and warriors. Like tigers are born to hunt, zebras are born to run, and cats are born to climb trees, they were born to work as instructed.

"It was exactly the purpose for which human beings were created. They were supposed to reproduce and expand on their own so that they could serve the Pangu alliance, their master, for all eternity, right?"

"It does sound very impressive."

Li Yao thought for a while and asked, "But just now, you said that the mud men were flawed, first-generation humans. What were their flaws?"

"Their lack of creativity and curiosity."

Zhuge Jinglun smiled and said, "Look, the Nuwa Clan had imprinted all knowledge into their genes. It was enough for them to deal with most of the animals and plants apart from the thirteen species of the Pangu alliance. They were almost perfect and invincible.

"So, they did not need curiosity or creativity. They acted based on their natural instincts like ignorant beasts, and they never knew the real significance of life.

"Maybe they could build a warship bare-handedly or transform a terrible planet into a habitable one, but they would never consider the significance of their work. It's like when a leopard pounced upon a zebra, the leopard was only thinking to live, and nothing else.

"What exactly is creativity? Creativity is the ability to make consecutive mistakes. You may make a hundred, a thousand, a million absurd mistakes, but one of those mistakes will lead to unexpected, amazing changes. That's evolution! That's creativity!

"The mud men did not have such an ability. Their knowledge and skills were all blessed. Their form bordered on perfection. They did not consider changes, nor was it necessary for them to change. They did not have curiosity, and nothing deserved their curiosity. If they were to reproduce for ten thousand years, the first generation and the last generation would be absolutely the same without any evolutions or mutations."

Li Yao gradually got it.

Inheriting talents through genes and maintaining curiosity and creativity were probably two incompatible directions of evolution.

"The Pangu Clan was very satisfied with the mud men, but the Nuwa Clan frowned at their product."

Zhuge Jinglun continued, "Perhaps a lot of extraterritorial devils had already found their way into the heads of the members of the Nuwa Clan. With their devotion to passion and chaos, how could they have liked the mud men?

"So, the Nuwa Clan spoke to the other thirteen primeval clans that the mud men had fatal flaws and were not suitable as servants for the Pangu alliance and that they needed new-generation human beings that were more balanced, independent, and creative.

"The Pangu Clan did not really like the idea, but since it was the Nuwa Clan's job to create human beings, they had to respect the Nuwa Clan's opinion and agreed to let them create a new batch of human beings.

"Just like that, the wise men, our earliest ancestors, were created.

"The deficiencies of the wise men are obvious compared to the mud men. Our ancestors were slim and prone to anger and fear. They were not even as strong as gorillas. Without tools, they could even be killed by tigers or lions. More importantly, the Nuwa Clan removed the naturally-endowed techniques in their genes. So, every wise man had to learn the necessary skills from their seniors on their own. Once they died, the skills they finally learned would be gone, and the newborn wise men had to start from scratch again."

"Now that you put it that way, it sounds like our ancestors were good for nothing!" Li Yao said, frowning.

"By the looks of it, yes."

Zhuge Jinglun smiled and said, "So, when the Nuwa Clan presented the 'new version' to the alliance, all the other twelve primeval clans were shocked and amused.

"Back then, all the thirteen primeval carbon-based lives had some naturally-endowed techniques and ancestral memories. Their talents were so powerful that their curiosity and creativity were suppressed, like how cancer cells occupy the living space of regular cells. So, none of them realized what terrifying power a brand-new life form, which did not have any naturally-endowed techniques but had the greatest curiosity and creativity, could unleash.

"Because we know nothing when we're born, we're forever curious about the wonderful new world around us. Because we learn our skills instead of inheriting them, we make mistakes, question the ancestors' experience, and challenge any authorities. Because we're so weak at the beginning, we crave to be stronger more badly than any other intelligent life. Our ancestors understood the true nature of 'human' even better than their creator did!

"But back then, the Pangu alliance did not know our power at all. However, because of the Nuwa Clan's insistence, several planets were selected as laboratories and the battle arena of the mud men and the wise men."

Attracted, Li Yao took a long breath of relief and mumbled, "Eventually, the wise men defeated the mud men."

"Yes, but there's more to it."

Zhuge Jinglun put on a weird smile again. "When the special experiment began, the mud men crushed almost all the wise men with their naturally-endowed techniques very easily, and the population of the wise men was reduced to 5% of the original in their worst days.

"However, the desire for exploration and survival modified the surviving wise men at an astonishing speed, allowing them to run crazily down on the path of evolution. They stole the mud men's magic equipment, learned their techniques, and modified those gears and techniques according to the features of their planet. Eventually, the wise men defeated the mud men on every planet and… ate them up.

"You should know what happened next, Black Wind King. The wise men proved that they were the most excellent tools. Though wary of the creativity and curiosity that the wise men revealed during the competition, the Pangu Clan was reassured when the Nuwa Clan proposed to bind them to the Path of Ultimate Benevolence. They manufactured human beings with the wise men as the template.

"However, part of the Nuwa Clan was already corrupted by the extraterritorial devils back then, and the Three Fundamental Laws were not as indestructible as they appeared. So, the awakening and rise of mankind were unstoppable."

"Is this just your hypothesis, or do you have any proofs?"

Li Yao asked, "Why does it sound like a tale to me?"

"Half half. The archaeologists and historians have found a lot of proofs that are like glittering stars in the sky."

Zhuge Jinglun said with a smile, "I'm merely connecting the stars into constellations."

"However, your story has a fatal flaw."

Pointing at the people of the Covenant Alliance down below, Li Yao said, "If the mud men were destroyed by the wise men in the experiments hundreds of thousands of years ago, how can those people be the mud men's offspring?

"As far as I know, many people of the Covenant Alliance were transformed from the normal people in the Imperium. Any normal person could be brainwashed and turned into a member of the Covenant Alliance. Are they all the offspring of the mud men?"

"No, no. no. I'm afraid that you didn't hear me right."

Zhuge Jinglun said calmly, "I didn't say 'destroyed'; I just said that the wise men swallowed and assimilated the mud men. They're both our ancestors, except that the genes of the wise men take up a greater percentage.

"You can understand that 90% of our genes are from the wise men and 10% are from the mud men, except that the genes from the mud men are usually suppressed. It's just like 'demons', another subspecies of mankind. We actually carry a lot of demon genes, and we can mutate into demons after we take Chaos water, can't we?

"In fact, there are so many mysteries in our genes that even the best geneticist can only translate 30% of our genes. They even believe that more than 50% of our genes are useless garbage.

"But is it possible? Does the successor of the primeval civilizations and the most perfect carbon-based life carry 50% of garbage genes? No, it's just an excuse for the specialists' ignorance. I believe that the 50% unknown genes are the hope of our evolution, which include part of the mud men's genes."

Chapter 2706: Ultimate Benevolence 19 days ago

"An interesting theory."

Li Yao remembered the body fundamentalist, a special profession among the Cultivators, who could activate their ancestral genes and change into primeval forms.

Li Yao himself had seen the primeval ancestors in his meditation and got some memories and heritages from them.

In other words, were there mud men genes in his body too? Interesting.

"It's true, because modern people all have their own different talents."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Some are good at refining, some at fighting, some at math, and some are naturally interested in geology or history. They also need studying, but they can study hundreds of times faster than other people can, and they can always distinguish themselves after the same training from the same teacher!

"It cannot be simply explained with a high IQ. Actually, it's the part of mud men genes that are still working. When the ancestors of those people were designed and created, they were intended to become the specialists of a certain field. Though hundreds of thousands of years have passed, those genes are still carrying out their original function."

Li Yao raised an eyebrow.

So, part of the reason why he could become a refining expert was the mud men genes in his body?

"When we understand the genes of the mud men, it will be easy to solve the puzzle of the birth of the Covenant Alliance!"

Zhuge Jinglun said excitedly, "When a stubborn soldier of the Imperium or a sentimental civilian is captured by the Covenant Alliance, the captive will be sent to a place that is known as Holy Temple.

"In the first step, the connection between the limbic system and the cerebral cortex in their head will be cut off so that their emotional thinking and their rational thinking will be completely separated.

"After such a surgery, the victim might still remember the things from the past, but they'll never have any feelings. They will be turned into absolutely heartless animals.

"The second step is to activate the mud men genes in their body, transform the modern victim into the mud men hundreds of thousands of years ago. In the meantime, their naturally-endowed techniques will be awakened so that the victim can go to work or the battlefield in no time.

"The third step. Since the mud men genes have been activated, it will be very easy to awaken the Three Fundamental Laws. After a series of hypnotization and brainwashing procedures, a loyal member of the Covenant Alliance will be produced!"

As they talked, they arrived near the town of the Covenant Alliance people.

Naturally, they did not enter the town but simply observed the Covenant Alliance people work inside through a transparent wall.

The wall had a lot of holes for air, so the Covenant Alliance people could also see them.

Li Yao found something very unusual.

Upon seeing them, many Covenant Alliance people in the farmland stopped working, and those who were enjoying the sun at the center of the town stood up and saluted them. Their eyes that were lackluster and indifferent were somehow filled with brilliance.

They were so passionate that even Li Yao almost couldn't take it anymore. He observed for a while, only to discover that the Covenant Alliance people were staring at Long Yangjun instead of him.

"Why are they looking at you in such a way?"

Li Yao said in a low voice, "It's creepy and terrifying!"

"Isn't it obvious?"

Long Yangjun replied with a low voice, "They've sensed the vibe of the Pangu Clan in me. I'm probably a 'demigod', or at least a higher servant of the gods, in their eyes."

Li Yao finally realized that Long Yangjun, half Pangu and half Nuwa, was indeed a demigod for the Covenant Alliance.

"So, they worship you and follow you?"

Interested, Li Yao thought for a moment and asked, "In that case, can you command them or even more people from the Covenant Alliance?"

"I know what you mean, but it's not as simple as that. If I could let all the Covenant Alliance people lay down their weapons with a simple word, I would've done it a long time ago!"

Long Yangjun glanced at Li Yao and said, "How can I explain the relationship between the Covenant Alliance people and their 'true god'? Their absolute obedience that is based on their genes is actually not so absolute. It's true that I can order those Covenant Alliance people to do some simple tasks, such as fetch water for me or show me the skill they're best at. That's not a problem.

"However, if my order is in violation of the Path of Ultimate Benevolence or will obviously harm the interests of the Pangu Clan, they will not carry out the order, and they might even turn against me."

"Huh?"

Blinking his eyes, Li Yao asked. "I still don't quite understand. Where exactly does the line lie?"

"It's very subtle. Let me give you an example—"

Pondering for a moment, Long Yangjun said, "The obedience that is based on genes is something like this. Imagine that you are an eighteen-year-old young man who is boiling with libido, and your girlfriend tells you that she will sleep with you as long as you do one thing for her. I trust that you will definitely get the thing done passionately as long as it's not against laws and morals, right?"

"About that…"

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, "I probably will do it even if it's slightly against laws and morals."

"That's right. The Covenant Alliance people's genetic instincts for the Pangu Clan is similar to an eighteen-year-old man's genetic instincts for his girlfriend."

Long Yangjun said solemnly, "After the Covenant Alliance people carry out the Pangu Clan's order, their brains will secrete a special matter that makes them even more exhilarated than after having sex. So, it's not hard to imagine how motivated they are.

"However, everything has boundaries. If this young man's girlfriend will not sleep with him unless he kills someone for her, it's definitely an unreasonable and outrageous request, and this young man might calm down and reevaluate his relationship with his girlfriend.

"By the same logic, if I give random commands as a 'demigod', those Covenant Alliance people will also suspect or hate me!"

"Now that you explained it with such a dirty metaphor…" Li Yao said, "It's much easier for me to understand. No wonder they're all staring at you lustfully."

Long Yangjun grinned and pointed at a guy. "Not just me. That guy over there is staring at you lustfully too!"

Briefly stunned, Li Yao looked at where Long Yangjun was pointing, only to discover that a captive was gazing at him in a very weird way and moving his eyes between Long Yangjun and him regularly.

"Seriously?"

Crept out, Li Yao rubbed the goosebumps on his arm hard. "What's this guy's problem? All the other people are staring at me, but he's the only one who's shifting between us. He doesn't seem very loyal!"

"It seems that Wuying Qi 3.0 was not lying, and the Sunspot Plan is a real thing."

Long Yangjun said, "This guy is likely a 'sunspot'. He's attracted to the air of Wuying Qi 3.0 in you and cannot tell which between us is his 'true god'!"

Li Yao was greatly enlightened.

Wuying Qi 3.0 did mention that a proportion of the Covenant Alliance people were the spies that were absolutely loyal to him. It was not unusual that some of them were captured and sent here.

Li Yao secretly memorized the captive's appearance and the number on his chest. He then asked Long Yangjun to pay special attention to him so that the captive wouldn't die.

After Li Yao completely controlled the Gold Crystal Pyramid, he could test the reliability of the Sunspot Plan with this captive.

"Director Zhuge…"

Thinking about the Sunspot Plan, Li Yao asked Zhuge Jinglun, "General Lei Chenghu suspected that the Covenant Alliance had to launch a strategic battle against the Imperium because it was bordering on collapse.

"As a specialist in Covenant Alliance, do you think it's possible? Will the Covenant Alliance, which seems to be in order, really collapse on its own?"

"That's definitely possible. Any high-precision system will inevitably collapse as it is scaled up!"

Zhuge Jinglun said, "To sustain a town of a thousand people and to sustain a civilization of a hundred billion people are completely different. There are too many problems for the latter.

"More importantly, the Covenant Alliance people are not 100% mud men. Most of them were transformed from the wise men. It's in our nature to love freedom and pursue new knowledge. How can that be completely erased? The shackle of the Three Fundamental Laws is not as solid as it seems!

"However, it's not likely that the Covenant Alliance will collapse on its own without the Imperium doing anything. After all, above Metal, Wood, Water, Fire, and Soil, there is another group named Ultimate Benevolence that is responsible for governance and cleansing. They will destroy all the factors that may lead to the collapse of the Covenant Alliance."

Chapter 2707: Connate Bodies 18 days ago

"Ultimate Benevolence?"

Recalling the Path of Ultimate Benevolence, Li Yao said, deep in thought, "Are they the ruling class in the Covenant Alliance?"

"You can say that, but it's not accurate."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Equality is appreciated in the Covenant Alliance. Theoretically speaking, everybody is a slave and a lamb of the gods. Nobody has the power to rule over the Covenant Alliance in the gods' place.

"However, to fight a war against the Imperium as an entity, administrators, commanders, order maintainers, and brainwashers are needed. Together, those people make up the group of Ultimate Benevolence.

"The group of Ultimate Benevolence is not exactly an independent group, but more like an approved and honorary title. When the people of Water, Wood, Metal, Fire, and Soil are filled with piety for the gods and realize their destinies, they can join the group of Ultimate Benevolence.

"Right, one of the important tasks for the group of Ultimate Benevolence is to maintain the Covenant Alliance's network. To some extent, the Covenant Alliance is like a beehive, and a powerful network is required to pass on the orders of the group of Ultimate Benevolence to the worker bees.

"The Cultivators, the Immortal Cultivators, and the common soldiers can fight on their own without the Spiritual Nexus or the orders from their superiors, but the Covenant Alliance people can't. If the connection between soldiers and commanders is cut off, it will be what you're seeing right now. The soldiers will not know what to do at all.

"So, the network is of paramount importance for the Covenant Alliance. Only the purest and most innocent candidates can become the network engineers of the Covenant Alliance.

"If it's like a beehive, there must be some sort of a 'queen' in it. They're on the top of the hierarchy in the Covenant Alliance!"

Interested, Li Yao asked again, "Is it possible for us to dissolve the Covenant Alliance by killing the guy on the top?"

"It's definitely possible, but we don't know who that person is exactly."

Zhuge Jinglun explained, "We only know of five 'Ultimate Benevolence Masters', from the groups Metal, Wood, Water, Fire, and Soil respectively. However, it's not easy to track them down. Even the most powerful fleets of the Imperium can barely lock onto the space fortresses they live in.

"Besides, judging from the social structure of the Covenant Alliance, if the five groups have an Ultimate Benevolence Master each, the ruling class should have an 'Ultimate Benevolence Master' too, and he should be the center of the Covenant Alliance.

"We don't know if the Covenant Alliance will collapse if we kill the sixth Ultimate Benevolence Master, because we've killed none of them in the past thousand years.

"However, the good news is that if the Covenant Alliance is really going to invade the Imperium, it will be our best opportunity to find the Ultimate Benevolence Masters and kill them."

"Huh?"

Li Yao was intrigued. "Why?"

"Because the Covenant Alliance's attack is highly dependent on the network."

Zhuge Jinglun said matter-of-factly, "If the Ultimate Benevolence Masters stay back in their bases, their orders have to pass dozens of Sectors before being delivered to the frontline. How can they win the war like that?

"So, in this final battle, all the Ultimate Benevolence Masters will definitely go to the frontline as the centers of the network with their invincible fortresses."

Li Yao nodded his head. Looking at the Covenant Alliance people who were almost identical, he asked, "If the Covenant Alliance people have naturally-endowed talents, do they need training? How can they be promoted into the group of Ultimate Benevolence?"

"Of course they have to train themselves. Only by training and meditating can they unlock more of their genes and receive the gifts of their ancestors!"

Zhuge Jinglun smiled and said, "But it's a mystery how one can be promoted into the group of Ultimate Benevolence. All that we know after hundreds of years of studies is that it's more likely for you to join the group of Ultimate Benevolence if you're Connate Bodies and that most of the Postnatal Bodies will be worker bees for their entire life. But it's not absolute. The Postnatal Bodies are the majority in the Covenant Alliance, and it's still hopeful for them to be promoted considering the absolute number."

"Connate Bodies? Postnatal Bodies?"

Li Yao asked, "Director Zhuge, are you talking about the different sources of the Covenant Alliance?"

"Yes, the Connate Bodies are the Covenant Alliance people that are 'manufactured' through artificial wombs."

Zhuge Jinglun explained, "In some sort of… man-manufacturing factories, the zygotes of mankind are screened and modified to make sure that the most excellent genes are gathered and the inferior genes are abandoned. As a result, the babies that are born in the end will be pure and powerful. Those are Connate Bodies."

Li Yao nodded his head. "I'm aware of that."

A hundred years ago, when Li Yao explored Kunlun, he had once encountered such a man-manufacturing factory, where he met the Three Fundamental Laws for the first time.

When the Three Fundamental Laws were imprinted on the cells and genes of the babies, it went without saying that those babies would be pure and pious.

To this day, Li Yao still remembered how the Immortal Cultivators who explored the land with him loathed the man-manufacturing factory and the Path of Ultimate Benevolence.

Nothing tarnished the sacredness of "human beings" more badly than a man-manufacturing factory did.

"The Connate Bodies created by the man-manufacturing factories are definitely the most perfect servants of gods. However, all the man-manufacturing factories under the Covenant Alliance's control are from the primeval age and have been worn out."

Zhuge Jinglun said, "Also, it seems that certain critical ingredients and technologies are required to manufacture human beings. All in all, the Covenant Alliance hasn't repaired all their man-manufacturing factories yet, and the Connate Bodies are being produced at a low efficiency with a high rejection rate.

"So, the Covenant Alliance has to acquire a new population through two new ways, namely natural reproduction and capturing slaves from the Imperium!

"The offspring of two Connate Bodies are purer and will be born with indestructible seals. However, the Connate Bodies are few in number in the first place, and they cannot give birth to a lot of babies even if they try their best. Besides, their children won't have any combat ability until they're mature, which will take a lot of time and resources.

"Capturing adult soldiers, workers, and farmers from the Imperium and brainwashing them will increase the Covenant Alliance's combat ability and weaken the Imperium's, which will mean double losses for the Imperium. So, although the Postnatal Bodies, who are created through brainwashing, are not as devoted and stable as the Connate Bodies, the Covenant Alliance has to count on them under the pressure of the war.

"The children born by the Postnatal Bodies are Postnatal Bodies too, and their reliability is also questionable. They have to be brainwashed by the group of Ultimate Benevolence again and again to ensure their loyalty to the gods.

"Right, Black Wind King, do you want to take a look at an instance of the group of Ultimate Benevolence?"

Li Yao raised an eyebrow. "You have a captive from the group of Ultimate Benevolence?"

"I do, and he's of quite a high level. However, he is not one of the Connate Bodies but only a Postnatal Bodies who betrayed the Imperium."

Zhuge Jinglun said proudly, "However, we may find more interesting information from the traitors than we can from the Connate Bodies, right?"

Li Yao was naturally eager to know more about the group of Ultimate Benevolence, who are the mysterious administrators, commanders, and priests.

This particular captive, however, was locked in a secret chamber even deeper in the island to make sure that not the slightest wave could be transmitted from his cell to the Covenant Alliance people's town up above, or a riot of the worker bees might be raised.

As they took the elevator to the bottom level of the island, Li Yao read the file on the captive via his mini crystal processor.

The captive was named Song Guanghe. He used to be a noble of the Song family, one of the four Kurfürst families in the Imperium. But he had never been at the core circle, or he would have needed to fight the battle in person.

When the Imperium's counterattack began, he was a colonel in the expedition army, and he went missing when his troop encountered the main forces of the Covenant Alliance in a skirmish.

Everybody thought that he had died. The Song family even applied for a medal and an honorary title for him.

However, a dozen years later, when the expedition army reclaimed the lost lands, they found him in a garrison of the Covenant Alliance. At that time, he had become the highest commander of all the ground troops of the Covenant Alliance on a resource planet!

Greatly embarrassed, the Song family had to claim that this Song Guanghe had mental derangement due to the enemy's torture and brainwashing, and he was sent to Zhuge Jinglun for studies and treatment.

Chapter 2708: The Brainwashed Colonel 18 days ago

"Song Guanghe is definitely not deranged. Besides, it was impossible for the Covenant Alliance to let a psychopath command the troops on a resource planet. His head is very clear, his logic is impeccable, and he remembers everything from his past."

Zhuge Jinglun explained to Li Yao, "He remembers his childhood and his youth as a noble in the Imperium, he remembers his wife and his three children, and he even remembers how he was modified in the Covenant Alliance, but he has converted himself to the Path of Ultimate Benevolence and completely cut himself from his past.

"Have a talk with him. It will be fun. I'm sure that you'll have a better understanding of the Covenant Alliance."

Li Yao met this Colonel Song Guanghe in a secret prison in the deepest part of the Third Research Institute.

The colonel left quite a good first impression on Li Yao. He was a tiny, graceful, and handsome man, wearing the hospital gown as if it were a military uniform.

He did not look as indifferent and heartless as the Covenant Alliance people up above. Instead, there was a warm and sunny smile on his face.

However, it was just an illusion.

According to Zhuge Jinglun, one nurse and one researcher had been deceived by the warm smile and killed by Colonel Song Guanghe.

Nobody saw that coming. The nurse and the researcher were unimportant. He couldn't escape even if he killed them. Besides, they did not torture him at all. In fact, they had been taking care of him. Nobody knew why he killed them.

After killing two innocent people in a row, Colonel Song Guanghe's cell was completely locked. Even the air would be filtered before it was transmitted into the room. The wall of the room was transparent on the outside so that everybody could see what Colonel Song Guanghe was doing but he could not see what was going on outside.

When Li Yao saw Song Guanghe, he was watching a soap opera with his hands on his knees like an obedient child. It was one of the programs designed for housewives and full of family dramas and struggles.

He didn't have a choice. As part of the study, Zhuge Jinglun played emotionally-stimulating stuff for him every day, hoping to detect abnormalities in his brainwaves.

But Song Guanghe's brainwaves were utterly unchanged although he was watching it wholeheartedly, as if he were staring at an empty wall.

On the nightstand nearby were a few novels and poem collections. Zhuge Jinglun did not want to give him access to crystal processors, so the novels and poem collections were rarely-seen in paper books. The smell of ink made the small prison cell smell fragrant.

It was another test.

But pitifully, though Song Guanghe read the novels and poems with great interest, his mind was absolutely tranquil.

"Colonel Song, I've brought a guest to you, a real big shot."

Through the broadcasting rune array, Zhuge Jinglun spoke to the prison cell.

"He would like to ask you a few questions, and if you answer them well, I may consider improving your living conditions and giving you a bigger room."

Song Guanghe watched for another half minute. It was not until the two women on the screen finished their fight that he finally moved his eyes back and slowly rose.

Although he could not see what was going on outside, his eyes were fixated on Li Yao through the wall.

"Director Zhuge, just call me Song Guanghe. It has been a long time since I was a colonel."

Song Guanghe smiled. "I would like to answer any questions from the honorable guest, but a different cell is unnecessary. You won't change a room for me. You just want to see me burst into rage.

"Besides, this is a nice place. Human beings are just grains of dust in the vast universe, and this room is spacious enough for a grain of dust."

Zhuge Jinglun looked at Li Yao and whispered, "Do you see how weird the group of Ultimate Benevolence is?"

Thinking for a moment, Li Yao said straightforwardly, "Colonel Song Guanghe, greetings. I'm very interested in the Covenant Alliance and the Path of Ultimate Benevolence. I'm even seeking another way apart from war to settle the conflicts between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance.

"Just now, I saw you watching a TV series that involves love and affairs. What do you think of it?"

"Not bad at all, my honorable guest."

Song Guanghe kept smiling. "It's very interesting."

"Very interesting?"

Li Yao asked in surprise, "How interesting? Can it touch your mind?"

"For example, we know that ants can construct a social system by communicating with each other through their antenna."

Song Guanghe said, "Assuming that we're ants, we'll be able to sense a lot of information through our antenna. We can feel the valiance and the fury of the ants. The battle of two groups of ants will be like a war between two countries. Too many epic heroes and unscrupulous villains will emerge. The legend might even be sung over for generations.

"Alas, we're not ants but humans. So, when we see the battle of two groups of ants under a tree, we'll find it interesting, but it's impossible for us to understand the heroes, the villains, and the legends of ants, right?

"It's the same for me. When I watch the TV series where two females are fighting over a male, it's like you watching two groups of ants battle. It's interesting, but nothing more."

If Song Guanghe could hear him, Li Yao would love to blow a whistle for him.

The group of Ultimate Benevolence was indeed different from the bottom-level Covenant Alliance people. They had their own logic that would not be rectified easily.

"Then…"

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao asked, "If the TV series cannot touch you, what about you in the past? Director Zhuge says that you remember your wife and your children very well. You can't feel anything special about them? How do you view yourself from the past?"

"Ah, myself from the past."

The smile on Song Guanghe's face was unchanged, as if it had been branded on him. He slowly shook his head and said, "It was truly a period of darkness that I would rather not look back at. Affected by the extraterritorial devils, my animal instincts, and the crazy people around me, I was mired in the swamp of sins.

"At that time, I was greedy about power. I craved for the appreciation of the experts, plotted against my partners, and exploited the weaklings.

"I killed my best friend for the leadership of a legion in my family. I married a woman I did not know in order to be promoted. I trained my children in the most extreme ways and asked them to take part in the most dangerous trials for my vanity, and my youngest child died because of that.

"Addicted to power and enslaved by the extraterritorial devils, I even slaughtered five hundred innocent villagers and pretended that they were the soldiers of the Covenant Alliance. I had to do that because it was what almost all the noble officers did.

"Sins. The boundless sins make me shudder in fear. I really don't know why I felt so comfortable when I was in a black swamp!

"Thankfully, all the sins are gone. The Ultimate Benevolence Masters have taught me the real truth and given me a chance to atone for my sins. Finally, I have found inner peace.

"Honorable guest, you must be an Immortal Cultivator too, aren't you? If so, you must be mired in the swamp of sins like I was in the past. I hope that you can realize your sins, get rid of the swamp, and find the ultimate peace that belongs to you one day."

Li Yao's eyes twitched. "Ultimate peace? Is it the reason why you killed the nurse and the researcher?"

"Yes, I sensed sins from them."

Song Guanghe said casually, "But more importantly, I saw that they still had a conscience and they were struggling in the black swamp not knowing how to get away, so I gave them a hand and led them to eternal peace."

"Don't you think it's against their wish?"

Li Yao asked, "You were brainwashed against your wish when you were captured, weren't you? What do you think of that? Do you not think that a man has the right to decide which path they should walk on?"

"If a man wants to cut his wrist, would you not kick the dagger in his hand away 'against his wish'?"

Song Guanghe asked back. "If a man is hanging himself, would you not take him down 'against his wish'? If someone is struggling in a fire pit, would you not save him until you ask him politely for his permission?

"Freedom? What freedom? Do human beings really should have the freedom to harm their bodies, to take drugs, to squander resources, or to brutalize one another?

"In the past, I was like a drug addict who waved a sharp blade in an abyss full of fire and venom, hurting myself, other people, and the whole universe. The Ultimate Benevolence Masters saved me and pulled me out of the abyss 'against my wish'. I was definitely forced, but isn't such force helpful and necessary?"

Chapter 2709: A Real Zealot! 17 days ago

Maybe it was Li Yao's illusion, but when Song Guanghe talked, he really saw a holy and pure fire burning around the man.

Song Guanghe's eyes were not dirty and lackluster like the eyes of the brainwashed and the deranged, but they were as clear as an enlightened Cultivator.

After a long time, Li Yao continued, "I'm very curious. How do you view your wife and your children? If they come here, would you help them… find eternal peace?"

"They're upset, in pain, and confused because of what happened to me."

Song Guanghe said, "My wife has been sleeping with the powerful men, hoping that they could restore her reputation. My children unleashed their fury on the people weaker than them. In my eyes, they have all been possessed by extraterritorial devils, and I certainly have to cleanse them if I cannot drive the devils away."

"Cleanse?"

Li Yao asked, "Let me confirm it again. You're saying that you will kill your wife and your children without feeling anything? They're pure strangers to you, like the nurse and the researcher were?"

"No, not strangers, but like siblings."

Song Guanghe said, "We're all the creatures, the slaves, the tools, and the lambs of the gods. My children were born through me and my wife by the gods' decree. So, they're the gods' children, not mine.

"We're siblings to each other. All humans are equal siblings. My wife, my children, and you… In my eyes, you're all the same. We're as intimate and equal to each other as we are to other people. If there's a chance, I will wake you up and save you without caring about the cost."

Taking a long breath, Li Yao said, "Colonel Song, I don't know if you still keep any logical thinking with you, but I still hope that you can consider calmly and rationally if your thoughts are based on your own will. Do you still have your own will?"

"Of course I do."

Song Guanghe said casually, "The gods' will is my will. There's nothing but the gods in the universe. My soul is never more serene and satisfied than when it's filled by the will of the gods."

"In other words, you're just a cell and a thought of the gods?"

Li Yao asked, "Have you ever considered the significance of such a life?"

"What about you, honorable guest, and millions of other Immortal Cultivators?"

Song Guanghe said, "You've been thrown into the furnace of desires since you were born. Not knowing that the extraterritorial devils live in your head, you struggle with desires for your whole life. You may die young of internal conflicts, but even if you live up to hundreds of years old, you cannot understand the real truth and will spend astronomical resources on the extension of your longevity. Yet, you will still end up in a tomb. What's the point of your life?

"Life is easier for you. What about the ordinary people oppressed by you? They struggle to survive below the ground like beasts, and they might never see any sunlight for their whole lives. They're not ants but more like moving mushrooms. For nothing more than keeping themselves from decaying, they have to weather through every second, every minute, and every day, but they will still end up in the darkness without leaving any mark. What's the point of their life?

"Maybe, for you, for us, and for them, life is just meaningless. Both the happiness of extravagance and the excruciating pain will perish in the passage of time, like water melting into a river that would eventually go dry. What's the point of anything? I don't know. Only the gods do."

"So, your purpose is to wake up the so-called 'gods', or the Pangu Clan?"

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao calmed himself down and asked the critical question.

"Yes. Our responsibility was to defend this universe while the gods were asleep so that everything would be maintained exactly as it had been."

Song Guanghe said without lying, "However, most human beings were corrupted by extraterritorial devils when the gods were asleep. They forgot about their sacred destiny and consumed the precious resources wickedly.

"Now that we're awakened, we're going to clean up the mess, end wars for good, restore peace and order, and wait for the gods to wake up.

"Trust me. I can feel it, so do all the Ultimate Benevolence Masters, that the gate of gods is about to be opened. The gods are returning from the hibernation of hundreds of thousands of years. Soon, they will reestablish their splendid civilization and bring us higher wisdom and instructions."

"You can feel it?"

Li Yao snorted. "You're placing the future of mankind on your feeling and the pity of the gods? How can you guarantee that the gods will bring guidance instead of slaughter, hope instead of destruction? You… We have gone out of control as 'tools'. It's a reasonable deduction that they will destroy the uncontrollable tools and develop new tools that are more reliable, right?"

"Don't predict what the gods will think with your evil mind that's corrupted by extraterritorial devils!"

Song Guanghe said, "How do you know that the gods will not bring guidance or hope? How do you know that human beings, who are evil, chaotic, and conceited, can live on in this dark and cold universe and weather through the greater tests? Chances are that we will be destroyed by ourselves before we step out of this tiny universe.

"You don't know that, and I don't either. We only believe what we're willing to believe. That's faith.

"If the gods really decide to annihilate mankind and replace us with more reliable tools, it will be our destiny that we can't and shouldn't avoid. Just face the judgment of the gods with no fear. Faith, instead of survival, should be the highest pursuit of intelligent creatures. Do our humble and sinful lives really matter compared to the decree of the gods?"

Song Guanghe couldn't have seen Li Yao, but when he talked, he was looking right at Li Yao's location perhaps by accident.

His tone had always been calm, without the devotion of a zealot, the condescension of a believer, or the slightest mockery. He simply talked about everything solemnly.

The calmness made Li Yao feel ignored. He was completely enraged.

"Do you know?"
