I saw our ancestors burning their lives and bringing colors to the dark and cold universe millions of years ago. I saw a glistening river of life that contained an infinite universe. I even saw the ultimate mysteries of the universe billions of years to come…”

“I can’t listen to him anymore.”

Boss Bai wasn’t in a good mood today, and his face was even more awful when Li Yao talked philosophically. “Li Yao, just tell it to us straight. How strong are you now? Can you blow up a starship with a punch? Can you take down a crystal suit legion on your own?”

“Of course I’m stronger than before, but that’s not important. Why can’t you understand?”

Li Yao said painfully, “I received the enlightenment of the ancestors and saw the mysteries of the universe! I’m starting to consider the ultimate questions like to be or not to be. I can’t wait to share my thinking with you, and all that you care about is how much stronger I am and whether or not I can blow up a warship?

“Can we consider the long term? If you’re only pursuing higher combat ability, you can create a few more crystal suit legions, starships, and fortresses, right? It’s not until today that I’ve finally realized that cultivation is a good and beautiful thing, and it’s so much more than a way to make you stronger. It’s about the ultimate communication with the universe! You’ve really disappointed me!”

Everybody looked and nodded at each other in bewilderment.

“It seems that you’re deranged after all.”

Boss Bai reached a conclusion coldly.

Everyone else nodded their head in agreement.

“…”

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao tried one last time to explain his current status in words that everybody could understand. “Assuming that all human beings were ants at the beginning, and the smartest and strongest of them turned into spiders, mantis, and centipedes through cultivation.

“In this process, their combat abilities became higher and higher, but no matter how they improved, they could still understand each other, and different levels could be classified clearly. For example, a mantis was stronger than a spider, and a centipede was stronger than a mantis. Also, those creatures pursued similar things that the ants were after, right?”

“Yes…”

Boss Bai said, “Then what?”

“Then, one of the ants suddenly had an epiphany and evolved into a butterfly during his cultivation.”

Li Yao said, “Do you think the butterfly is stronger than a spider, or is it the other way around? No, no, no. The comparison of combat abilities is meaningless. The butterfly does not care about combat ability because what it pursues is entirely different from what the insects on the ground are after. Entirely different! Do you see what I mean?”

“I do.”

Boss Bai nodded and said, “You’re saying that although you wasted infinite resources, almost killed me, the Fist King, and Long Yangjun, raised appalling views in the sky of the capital, and delayed the march of our army, your combat ability is fundamentally unimproved. You’re just a butterfly that can only trigger cheesy sound and visual effects.

“However, while your combat ability has not improved, your level is much higher than before, and unlike the mortals like us, you’re starting to consider the ultimate questions of the universe. You can’t explain it in a way that the idiots like us can understand, but your vision is way greater than ours. Is that what you’re saying?”

“Well…”

Li Yao said, “If you have to put it that way, I guess I have to agree with you, but there’s no need to be frustrated. Really, you’re my best friends and my family. I’ll definitely share what I felt with you. I believe that you will definitely make it to the Divinity Branching Stage someday. By then, you’ll realize the feeling of turning from an ant into a butterfly and stop caring about combat ability.”

“…”

Everybody was silent for a long time. Boss Bai said, “I’m thinking of beating him up. Is there any objection?”

“No.”

“Agreed,” said Lei Chenghu gloomily.

“You can do that.” Li Linghai nodded her head expressionlessly.

Li Jialing and Long Yangjun looked at each other. Then, they raised their heads and stared at the dome of the camp without saying a word.

Chapter 2719: Paradox Of Supermen

“No, you can’t do that. I’ve just finished a training session. I’m still very weak!”

Li Yao sweated hard. “Besides, I’ve just climbed to an amazing new level and perceived the mysterious truth of the universe. Can you give me some respect?”

“Alright, feel free to fight back. It’s the best way to figure out your maximal combat ability.”

Boss Bai rubbed his fists and grinned hideously. “I don’t believe that you can become invincible after training in a cave for seven days and seven nights. How can it be so simple? Let’s see how good you are in a real test!”

“You’d better drop it. I can more or less understand what Li Yao just said. It was not purely nonsense.”

Right when everybody gazed at Li Yao and was ready to beat him up, Long Yangjun finally said, “If you’re a hundred years ahead of your age, you will be a genius, but if you’re a thousand years ahead, you will be a complete lunatic. That’s the same for cultivation.

“Our bodies contain infinite heritages and powers from the ancient times, but most of the heritages and powers are beyond our comprehension. They’re not prepared for us at all, but for our offspring in the future. Even if Li Yao has activated those heritages in advance, it’s not of actual significance for mankind today.

“The example that Li Yao gave on ants was not a bad one. If an ant learns of the ways a mantis attacks or can release the venom of a scorpion, it will be great, but if what the ant learns is how a tiger pounces and how an eagle flies, would it be of any significance for a creature as weak as an ant?

“It is needless to say that energy and matter must be balanced and that everything comes with a cost. How much damage will be caused to an ant’s soul and body if it wants to awaken the skills of a tiger? How much energy will it need to consider the flight of an eagle? So, it might not be an excuse that Li Yao said that he’s weak. Didn’t we see how he looked like a fossil a few days ago?”

“Well…”

Everybody looked at each other.

Apparently, Long Yangjun was much more trustworthy than Li Yao. Everybody was deep in thought now that she endorsed Li Yao.

“It’s a paradox that nobody can escape.”

Not caring if other people could understand, Long Yangjun continued casually, “For various reasons, we all want to turn ourselves into ‘supermen’. Sometimes, it’s for fame; sometimes, it’s for fortune; sometimes, it’s for power; and sometimes, it’s for revenge. We try our best to cultivate ourselves exactly to make ourselves ‘supermen’.

“However, have you considered that if we do become supermen one day, will the reason that motivated us at the beginning still matter?

“If a superman is bound by the pursuits of fame and fortune or by their hate, unable to free themselves, would they deserve to be called supermen, and could they unleash the power of a superman? No, bound and confused by those things, they cannot become supermen at all.

“I’m sure that you know what happened when the ancient Cultivators reached high levels. They were all gone from reality.

“Most of them really died because of their inappropriate operations, but is it possible that some of them really cut off their connections to this world and ascended to universes of higher levels without being bound by this world anymore?

“Maybe death is never the destination but the beginning of another brand-new journey, which Li Yao has peeped at. To be honest, I’m very glad that he did not turn into butterflies or rainbows when he was attracted by the landscape in the new journey and was still willing to wake up and fight for this world.”

What Long Yangjun said dumbfounded the emperor, the empress dowager, the general, and the mechanical lives.

Even Li Yao was astounded himself; his jaw hitting the ground.

“Wow…”

It was not until a minute later that Li Yao finally murmured, “If I weren’t Li Yao, I would’ve thought that you had conspired with Li Yao so that you could flatter each other with pure crap!”

“President Long!”

Li Linghai, however, said deep in thought, “You became weird after you touched Li Yao in the Gold Crystal Pyramid, and you had been in a trance in the past couple of days. However, when I met you this morning, I felt that you were reborn. Besides, what you described just now seemed to be a personal feeling. Have you also made a breakthrough?”

“Maybe.”

Long Yangjun smiled. “I don’t like to play mysterious, but even if I did have a breakthrough, it’s not something that can be explained in human languages. I can only say that I saw something that had never been seen and received certain heritages that are too advanced to be used.”

“If the heritages are very advanced…”

Li Linghai asked, “How can they not be used?”

“Imagine that the primitive apes received ways to calculate the mass of a star based on the orbital changes of planets.”

Long Yangjun said, “Would those ways be of any use for the apes however accurate they were?”

Li Linghai frowned, lost for words.

“If we’re not so egoistic as to claim that we’re the destination of evolution…”

Long Yangjun said, “Then, we will have to admit that 99% of the secrets buried deep inside our genes were not prepared for us but for mankind ten thousand or even a million years later. It’s neither necessary nor possible to finish mankind’s journey in the next million years within a hundred years, no?”

“Got it.”

Li Linghai took a deep breath and smiled bitterly. “I was too stubborn.”

“Hey, Your Highness, why do you appear so enlightened?”

Li Yao’s eyes bulged as he complained, “Why do I feel that you were all suspicious when I said it but convinced when Long Yangjun said exactly the same things?”

Everybody was amused.

“Seems so.”

Li Jialing said, “I don’t know why, but what Sister Long said was reasonable and enlightening, whereas Brother Yao seemed to be bragging about himself. It’s probably a matter of image, because Brother Yao does not look like someone who will ascend at all.”

“That’s definitely one way to look at it.”

Long Yangjun added casually, “Although Li Yao peeped at the path to the Higher Truth and got a chance to enter universes of higher levels, he was too stupid, and he couldn’t cut off his connections with the mortal world, so he was kicked back by the Higher Truth.”

“Now that you put it that way, Sister Long…”

Li Jialing nodded quickly. “It’s much more reasonable!”

Li Yao was angry at first, but hearing Long Yangjun’s theory, he couldn’t help but smile too.

“That’s probably exactly what happened.”

He scratched his head and spat. “I’m not interested in the universes of higher levels at all. Would life as a ‘superman’ really be better than my life right now? Even if I am to see what the Higher Truth looks like one day, I’ll at least have to settle down my family, friends, and compatriots in this place before I embark on new adventures, right?”

“Damn right, Brother Yao!”

Li Jialing finally lunged at him and hugged Li Yao. The vibe of an emperor that the young lion had developed for half a month was completely gone, and he became Li Yao’s most beloved younger brother again. “Welcome back! Before you explore the Higher Truth, you’d better help us mortals resolve the imminent problems first!”

“Exactly, Black Wind King.”

Lei Chenghu’s brows were still furrowed. “The situation is very tricky right now, and the newborn regime of the reformists will collapse after one moment of carelessness. I’m sure that you do not want the four families to reclaim the power, do you?

“Although you said that your combat ability did not significantly increase, you caused quite a view outside of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Too many people awakened their spiritual roots and saw illusions because of your influence. So, you must’ve acquired some new techniques, haven’t you?”

“Well, of course I have.”

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, “Now, the range of my vitality field has been significantly expanded. With the enhancement of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, it may be able to cover a hundred square kilometers. In this range, my vitality field can influence and boost the natural magnetic field and show unusual phenomena. If the people whose brainwave frequency matches mine, they will feel comfortable and even live a longer life after they enter the range!”

“…”

Lei Chenghu asked, “That’s all?”

“That’s not enough?”

Li Yao asked, “In the age of the ancient Cultivators, my ability would’ve been as miraculous as bringing the dead back to life, and I would’ve been respected as a real deity!”

Chapter 2720: Shared Brain!

“Fine, dear… ‘deity’, not that I’m dissatisfied with your abilities…”

Taking a deep breath, Lei Chenghu rubbed his nose tiredly and said, “But we’re really in the middle of a crisis. Other than making other people comfortable and living a longer life, do you have any techniques that are more… direct?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao thought carefully for a moment and said, “Now, I’m ten times more sensitive to vitality fields and brainwaves than I was in the past. I can easily see through someone else’s vitality field and even interfere with their brainwaves by invading their cerebral nerves.

“It’s like how a great expert of crystal processors can hack someone else’s crystal processor, change the data in it, transmit information, or paralyze it with a virus.

“Of course, the human brain is a hundred times more complicated than crystal processors. The experts whose souls are powerful and determined have dozens of firewalls around their brains, and it’s impossible for me to get in their heads.

“However, it’s possible for me to influence the general experts and the ordinary people. The simplest way is to invade their optical and auditory nerves so that they will see and hear things that do not exist.”

Lei Chenghu was still frowning. “Like a group of dancing butterflies? How can that help us?”

“More than butterflies!”

Li Yao blinked his eyes and looked at Lei Chenghu. Suddenly, glittering gold threads that were invisible to ordinary people spread out of Li Yao’s head and stretched out to Lei Chenghu, tying up his head.

Lei Chenghu’s eyes turned weird, and he suddenly jumped as if a scorpion had just bitten him.

“What are you doing?” He rumbled in fury.

Few people had ever seen Lei Chenghu so anxious when he usually kept calm in the greatest emergencies. They were all shocked.

“Hiss!”

Lei Chenghu was an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage anyway, and his soul was one of the strongest ones in the Imperium. In his fury, his flames of spiritual energy burst out and banished Li Yao’s brainwaves, forcing Li Yao to cry and hold his left eye.

When he opened his hand, his left eye was as red as blood as if someone had just punched it.

Lei Chenghu, on the other hand, was pale, as if he had been frightened.

“Did you invade Lei Chenghu’s optical nerves?”

Long Yangjun realized what had happened. “What illusions did you project to his retina to make him so frightened? It can’t be butterflies, can it?”

“Not butterflies or caterpillars, nor scorpions or centipedes.”

Li Yao rubbed his red and swollen eye and smiled as he added, “It’s just a countdown.”

“A countdown before a man’s eyes that appears out of nowhere and cannot be rid of?”

Long Yangjun mumbled, “It does sound scary!”

“But I’m still too weak. I only succeeded because I attacked General Lei Chenghu when he was unprepared and when he was too tired from work.”

Li Yao opened his hands and said, “The moment General Lei Chenghu realized it and raised his wariness, my invasion collapsed, and even my own soul was hurt.”

“But few people in the universe are as strong as General Lei Chenghu.”

Long Yangjun said, deep in thought, “The secondary experts and the ordinary people will barely be able to resist your invasion.

“Imagine that an ordinary person suddenly sees a glittering countdown that follows him wherever he goes. He can even see it in the darkness when he closes his eyes and sleeps.

“In the meantime, he hears your nasty voice all the time. Wow. I think that this ordinary person will only end in two ways. He will either go crazy or worship you as a god.”

“I think so too.”

Li Yao smiled bitterly. “It’s not until today that I’ve finally realized what the deities in the ancient times are about. When they reached a certain level where they could influence other people with their vitality fields, it was very easy for them to find their believers and create a nonexistent heaven!”

Silently, everybody was deep in thought.

As the smartest people in the Imperium of True Human Beings, they certainly knew that combat ability was not all that mattered in a battle and that this massively-manipulative technique was critical.

“Come to think of it, it’s possible that Wuying Qi had influenced everyone without them realizing it like Li Yao did before he became Blackstar the Great that dominated the universe!”

Looking at Lei Chenghu, Li Linghai said, deep in thought, “General Lei Chenghu, we used to worship Blackstar the Great, but was it a genuine feeling from our hearts, or were we deceived by Wuying Qi’s vitality field without us realizing it?”

Briefly stunned, Lei Chenghu became even more gloomy.

“That’s right. This technique would be the most marvelous weapon for an ambitious schemer.”

Li Yao paused for a moment and said unconcernedly, “But for me, it’s of little use. I’ve never planned to become a supreme god, which would be too tiresome a job. I only want to be free and happy, and I want everybody in the world to be the same.

“On the other hand, the connection between cerebral nerves is bidirectional. When my virtual cerebral nerves invade someone else’s brain, our brains will be connected. The things they see, hear, and think will be transmitted to me. I can even communicate with him through telepathy.

“It means that if I can enter someone’s head, I can see what he sees and hear what he hears even if he’s thousands of kilometers away. I can even talk to him remotely without any communication magic equipment.

“Of course, it’s just a theoretical possibility. After all, I’ve only just awakened the ability, and I need more tests to figure out the success rate of ‘vision sharing’ and ‘mind communication’.”

“What a person sees and hears thousands of kilometers away can be known by Brother Yao simultaneously?”

Li Jialing was shocked. “It’s almost like the ancient tales where the experts can see and hear things far away.”

“That’s right. I estimate that those techniques in ancient tales are quite similar to mine.”

Li Yao said, “Even if your eyes are as keen as the most advanced telescope, how can you see what’s behind a high wall through the blockage?

“Ears are even less reliable. If you want to hear a sound thousands of kilometers away, there will be too many interferences and noises. How can you filter them and decode the sound that you want?

“So, you cannot see and hear things far away with your eyes and ears. Instead, you see and hear them indirectly by sharing someone else’s vision and hearing.

“It’s nothing marvelous. Both sounds and pictures will be turned into biophysical signals when they enter the brain, and the signals are being sent to the outside world as brainwaves every second. They’re like the ripples of a lake that is never tranquil, except that the ripples are too feeble for us to detect.

“But on the other hand, as long as I can sense the ripples, I’ll be able to infer the pictures that caused them. It’s as simple as that.”

“Really? So simple?”

Li Jialing thought for a moment and shut up.

“So, it’s possible for you to see and hear everything that someone in the Covenant Alliance can?”

Lei Chenghu immediately realized the military application of this technique. “For example, you can see what the counselors and guards around those Ultimate Benevolence Masters see? No, you can simply enter the head of an Ultimate Benevolence Master and see what he sees?”

“How’s it possible?”

Li Yao exclaimed, “Firstly, the five or six Ultimate Benevolence Masters of the Covenant Alliance must be experts in mental techniques, and they must be protecting their heads more meticulously than you did, General Lei. It’s possible that they might launch a counterattack and absorb everything in my brain, turning me into their puppet, if I am to invade their brain recklessly!

“Secondly, we have no idea where those bloody Ultimate Benevolence Masters are hiding at all. The universe is too vast a place for me to search. It must be noted that by extending my brainwaves, my soul will be essentially out of my body, and it’s exhausting. Think about the difficulties and risks to search for several Ultimate Benevolence Masters dozens of Sectors away across the universe! Chances are that my soul will be blown apart by a space storm, leaving my empty shell at the bottom of the Gold Crystal Pyramid!”

Pondering for a moment, Lei Chenghu asked, “Then, what kind of people of the Covenant Alliance can you get in touch with?”

“The sunspots.”

Li Yao said, “Now, the Thick Soil Sector is a place where a lot of people of the Covenant Alliance have gathered. There must be a lot of ‘sunspots’ that Wuying Qi 3.0 mentioned in there. Those people are the easiest for me to find out and approach. I’ll take them as platforms while I search for the Ultimate Benevolence Masters, figure out the Covenant Alliance’s plan of attack, try to decapitate the Ultimate Benevolence Masters, and even raise a storm within the Covenant Alliance!”

Chapter 2721: The Anti-Rebel Army Takes Off!

“That will be for the best!”

Li Jialing stood up excitedly and waved his fist. “The Covenant Alliance is half a collective civilization. They’re more dependent on the network and the leaders than we are. If we can decapitate those Ultimate Benevolence Masters, the Covenant Alliance will definitely collapse! Brother Yao, please try your best!”

Li Yao nodded his head and turned to Lei Chenghu and Li Linghai. “What’s going on in the frontline? Did the Covenant Alliance take any action?”

Lei Chenghu shook his head and said that the Covenant Alliance hadn’t done much yet since they attacked the Thick Soil Sector.

Li Yao had only secluded himself for seven days, and it was impossible to occupy a Sector so quickly. It was even barely possible to completely swallow the main planet of this Sector.

The Covenant Alliance had only occupied the strategic locations and jump points in the Thick Soil Sector and destroyed the Imperium’s starships in space. It was possible that some survivors were still resisting the intruders in the trenches and the abandoned mines on the planets. “Surrender” was not an option in the war between the Imperium and Covenant Alliance. Unless they were injured and captured, the soldiers of the Imperium would fight until they died.

So, although the massive resistance came to an end on day one, the battle would still last for quite a while, and it was impossible for the Covenant Alliance to clear the Sector without a few months.

Based on the Imperium’s investigation, the Covenant Alliance was not interested in fully occupying the Thick Soil Sector. They were only using the Thick Soil Sector as a platform for their next attack.

The most direct evidence was that the main forces of the Covenant Alliance hadn’t been detected yet. That fleet disappeared again after it crushed the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector unstoppably.

The few Sectors around the Thick Soil Sector, however, reported the scouts of the Covenant Alliance. Obviously, the Covenant Alliance’s troops were about to spread out.

“According to the general staff of the expedition army, the Covenant Alliance is not aiming for any of the Sectors or resourceful planets in the newly-recovered area. If they really care about those places, they wouldn’t have retreated so quickly in the beginning.”

Pointing at a map that was projected by his mini crystal processor, Lei Chenghu said, “The Covenant Alliance’s real target is the manpower and resources that the Imperium has devoted to the newly-recovered area.”

“Ever since the victory of the Imperium’s counterattack, the Imperium has sent countless engineering and construction troops to the newly-recovered area, ready to rebuild the planets that the Covenant Alliance burnt to the ground. Many sects and enterprises have also invested great capitals in the area.

“Now that the Covenant Alliance has come back and occupied the Thick Soil Sector, which is a transportation hub, the connection between the frontline and the rear is disrupted. If the Covenant Alliance occupies another few hubs, our connection with the frontline will be completely cut off.

“I’m not saying that we can’t directly send carriers to the frontline from the Empyreal Terminus Sector, but it will consume too much more fuel than we can bear. Perhaps, to send one ship of fuel to the frontline, ten ships of fuel will be consumed for the journey. We do not have so many resources for us to squander at this point!

“So, if the Covenant Alliance’s strategy works out, they will be holding our soldiers, people, and assets in the frontline as their hostages, and they will be able to launch a real attack when our people are anxious!”

Looking at the map, Li Yao asked, deep in thought, “Then, what countermeasures can the Imperium take?”

“Regretfully, there is little we can do although we’re aware of the Covenant Alliance’s strategy.”

Gritting his teeth, Lei Chenghu said coldly, “We’re terribly outnumbered, and we can only deal with one enemy at a time. If we split our troops and fight the Covenant Alliance as well as the four families simultaneously, we’ll suffer brutal failures on both battlefields.

“So, we have to clench all our troops into an unstoppable fist and blow up the head of one of the enemies first, before we blow up the second one with the high morale from the first victory!

“So, even if we know that the noose on our neck is tighter and tighter, we don’t have any choice except to carry out the strategy that we agreed upon in the beginning. We’ll gather all our forces, including the Astounding Thunder Fleet, the Deep Sea Fleet, the imperial guards, the Arsonist Fleet, etc., and finish off the four families first.

“While we march to the nest of the four families, the expedition army has to resist the Covenant Alliance on their own. I just hope that they can make it!”

Li Yao was silent.

He had heard the pressure on the God of War between his lines.

No wonder the seasoned general of the Imperium whose nerves used to be as tough as iron was so easily invaded by him.

There had never been a perfect choice in any battlefield. Most of the time, people had to take chances.

Whether or not their decision was correct, and whether it would save a country or destroy it… Those were questions that could only be answered with time!

Even Li Yao couldn’t argue that Lei Chenghu’s ruthlessness to let the expedition army resist the main forces of the Covenant Alliance was wrong. He knew that splitting troops in a war was terrible.

Li Yao looked at Boss Bai.

Boss Bai nodded and said, “I second General Lei’s strategy. But there’s still a critical problem, which is our internal coordination.

“It must be noted that, although a lot of troops are gathered in the Empyreal Terminus Sector, they’re from complicated sources. Some are from the expedition army, some are from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, some are from the Cultivators, and some are from the ace fleets of the four families that surrendered.

“The troops seem overwhelming, but they’re actually disorganized. If you deal with the main forces of the Covenant Alliance with such an army, it’s possible that the army will crash when the war doesn’t go well.

“How can we reorganize loose troops into an unstoppable army? The simplest approach is victory! We will polish the new armies with consecutive victories and make up for all the internal disagreements with burning morale!

“After losing their best fleets, the four families are now unprecedentedly weak even though they boast a large territory. Our morale will be higher than ever after we completely vanquish the four families. Then, we will turn around and fight a strategic battle against the Covenant Alliance. We will be utterly unstoppable by then!”

Li Yao nodded his head. He had to admit that Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai were much more thoughtful than he was.

“Right…” Li Yao asked again, “What about the federation? Did the parliament make any decision?”

“Not yet.”

With a vague smile, Boss Bai said, “However, we received a letter from Bai Kaixin, the chief of staff of the Burning Prairie Fleet. The federation is now establishing a new rapid response troop based on the Burning Prairie Fleet. You know that the federation will be influenced by this war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance whether or not it takes part in the war. So, it can’t be wrong to make preparations first.”

“So, the federation is making preparations for the war too!”

Li Yao clenched his fists and remembered the fearless Ding Lingdang again.

He knew that the Star Glory Federation would not sit around!

“Got it! You can focus on eliminating the rebels of the four families!”

His eyes glowing, Li Yao declared with confidence, “I’ll try my best to find out those pretentious Ultimate Benevolence Masters. Chances are that I can even deliver the butterflies in my head into the Covenant Alliance and raise an astounding storm there!”

…

Now that Li Yao safely concluded his training, there was no reason for the anti-rebel army to delay. It set off from the capital at night, and the dazzling exhaust flames from the starships almost tore the night sky apart.

The communication and cooperation among human beings were amazing.

A year ago, Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators regarded each other as sworn enemies.

But now, the Immortal Cultivators’ Astounding Thunder Fleet and the Cultivators’ Arsonist Fleet were working together against a common foe, and the Immortal Cultivators’ capital had to be defended by battle puppets and fully-automatic defense positions that were under the control of Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King, who were the next generation of life.

Could Immortal Cultivators really trust Cultivators?

Could Cultivators really trust Immortal Cultivators?

Could Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators 100% trust Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King?

The answer was definitely no.

They all had concerns. They were all wary and vigilant of each other. They all couldn’t help but consider the worst possible outcome. They all sighed at night when nobody was around.

However…

The universe was too vast and dark, and they had no choice except to find warmth in each other’s arms and press forward!

Chapter 2722: Tomorrow Will Come

Three days passed.

Li Yao had been really busy during the three days.

At first, he thoroughly examined his body and studied his brain in the best hospital in the Imperium, which was also the best hospital that human beings ever had.

Then, he modified a brainwave magnifier according to his brainwave burst index before installing it in the control room of the Gold Crystal Pyramid as a launcher of the Sunspot Plan.

The good news was that the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance were still fighting in the newly-recovered area. Although their main forces never showed up, their scouts and cyber troops hadn’t rested for a single second.

The Covenant Alliance was highly dependent on the Spiritual Nexus, so the Imperium had deployed plenty of troops to infiltrate the Covenant Alliance’s network.

From the capital to the frontline, a high-speed channel of information on the Spiritual Nexus had been established.

Through the enhancement of the brainwave magnifier and the Gold Crystal Pyramid, it was theoretically possible for Li Yao to travel through space and reach the frontline.

However, it was highly risky and was similar to a ghost traveling through a solar storm. His soul might be gone forever after one moment of carelessness.

“Dad, are you really ready?”

Deep inside the Gold Crystal Pyramid, Xiaoming and Wenwen offered protection to Li Yao as usual.

To some extent, those two kids were probably the greatest beneficiaries of the reform. Neither Li Yao nor Li Jialing had improved as much as they did.

Before the reform, they were two panicked kids hiding down below in the capital planet. Even though they found a secret base, it was just debris from thousands of years ago, and their bodies and their brains were just rusty garbage and wasted crystal processors.

But right now, to make up for their shortage of hands and to better coordinate their resources, the reformists were forced to entrust the defense of the capital to them.

They could connect the most advanced super crystal processors of the Imperium of True Human Beings as their new brains, and they could control thousands of the most powerful and cruel iron puppets as an extension of their bodies.

The Imperium of True Human Beings’ databases that were a hundred times more complicated than those in the Star Glory Federation were the most delicious food for them. They swallowed all the data they could reach greedily one planet and one Sector after another.

They were growing a hundred times faster than Li Yao did. They had unexpected changes, brought new surprises to Li Yao, and became more sophisticated every day.

To be honest, had it not been for their advancement as well as the Fist King’s, it would’ve been impossible for Li Yao, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, and the people of the Imperium to figure out the mysteries of the Gold Crystal Pyramid and put the Sunspot Plan into action so quickly. They couldn’t even have woken Li Yao up!

Li Yao had never suspected their kindness and sincerity, but sometimes… Or rather, every morning when he woke up, he couldn’t help but feel surprised at their growth.

He had intentionally or unintentionally asked Li Jialing, Li Linghai, and Lei Chenghu whether they were really comfortable with entrusting the new-generation information lives to protect their home. Weren’t they worried that the Wisewares would rebel, as depicted in many lousy fictions?

The three Immortal Cultivators’ answers were the same.

They said that they were not unafraid, but they had to do what they must for the victory.

Sometimes, when they were too thirsty, one had to drink the poison even though they knew that it was poison.

Li Yao had no idea why the two kids had been growing so fast, or whether it was really a bliss that they controlled massive resources in the capital so easily, just like he did not know whether or not his ability of mind-splitting would be a disaster.

He could only reassure himself that the evolution of mankind was unstoppable and so was the birth of mankind’s successor, just like how the primeval civilizations, which were powerful and developed, failed to stop mankind from rising.

Lives would find a way out on their way, and tomorrow would inevitably come.

“I’m ready. Let’s get started.”

Li Yao came back to himself and smiled at the two little kids.

Now, the kids looked entirely different in his eyes.

They were still in the shapes of silly puppets, mechanical spiders, or even crystal processors and computation chips that were embedded in the wall, but Li Yao could see glowing sparks in the cold iron and sense the air of life in the sparks.

The sparks were exactly the heartbeats and breaths of his children.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao sat down on the glittering throne of the master of the Gold Crystal Pyramid that he made specifically for himself.

Immediately, several hollows syringes were stabbed into his veins, injecting nutrition drugs that contained powerful energy.

Then, an iron hat to which a lot of marrow crystals were stuck descended and covered his head.

“Dad, Wenwen and I will closely monitor your breath, heartbeat, brainwaves, and other parameters. If anything goes wrong, we will wake you up by force.”

Xiaoming said again solemnly, “However, what you’re about to do is not recorded in any of our databases, so it will be hard for us to decide if something is wrong. If your soul is away for too long and its connection to your body is cut off, you will become a ghost.

“Besides, this journey will be even more dangerous than when you just arrived in the Imperium and landed on the Land of Sins. At that time, you placed your soul in an iron puppet, but this time, you’re going to enter a living body, and you may be swallowed by them after one moment of carelessness. Even if you’re not, you may be influenced by their brainwaves and become a stranger. Are you aware of that?”

“I am.”

Li Yao said, “Bring it on!”

“Okay. Initiating the first experiment of the Sunspot Plan!”

Xiaoming and Wenwen activated the brainwave magnifier and the Gold Crystal Pyramid at the same time. Immediately, the whole launch room glowed, and every marrow crystal unleashed the most brilliant light as if they were exploding while they enshrouded Li Yao and leaked into his pores.

Li Yao felt that he was turning into a rainbow and shooting into the sky.

“Ahhhhhhhhhhh!”

Li Yao cried hysterically. His body was heavier and heavier, but his soul was lighter and lighter. His body and his soul were separated, as if his body were no longer a protective shell but a hard and stinky cage. He could not tolerate the clumsy, heavy, sticky, and smelly place anymore and jerked like a lobster falling into boiling oil.

Shua!

Li Yao felt that his skull was breaking open and his soul was passing through a colorful and revolving channel. Faster and faster, he was launched by the Gold Crystal Pyramid into the sky of the capital!

The feeling was quite hard to describe.

It was said that when people died, their souls would float in midair and stare at their bodies as well as their families who were wailing next to the bodies.

Li Yao had similar feelings at this moment, except that the feelings were a hundred times more detailed and abundant.

He was like an invisible but almighty god that was drifting above the most prosperous business district of the capital.

Down below were skyscrapers that were ablaze with lights. It was the most beautiful night view.

Even so, what Li Yao could see was much more than that.

He could see the waves of the Spiritual Nexus, which connected the city like colorful bridges.

He could see that every plaza was releasing tremendous radiations that were in different layers because of their different materials.

He could see the long exhaust flames of the shuttles in the sky, whose glamor could never be caught by ordinary people.

Of course, what occupied most of his vision was the radiations from the sun that had fallen, the moon that had just risen, and the capital planet itself, as well as the natural magnetic field made of the congregating radiations.

Li Yao had never appreciated the beauty of the planet of the universe in such a way. He was fascinated.

Li Yao smiled. Feeling like a drop of water that was falling into a river, he jumped agilely on the Spiritual Nexus and transmitted himself quickly.

At a speed almost close to that of light, Li Yao’s soul rushed forward in a brand new form of life in the unknown realm.

Chapter 2723: May I Come In?

The closer he was to the city, the more Li Yao sensed the burning fire of life that was blossoming like flowers and sending all kinds of sounds and pictures to Li Yao.

Of course, he could not directly pry into other people’s hearts. But when they were too excited, it was possible that their brainwaves would reveal some of their feelings and be received by Li Yao.

Those complicated memory pieces crushed Li Yao’s soul again and again like the glamorous images on a kaleidoscope.

It was so overwhelming that Li Yao did not feel enjoyable as a peeper at all but was tortured. As if he were involved in a tsunami of cries, shrieks, roars, and sobs, he was torn apart into pieces, before he sank into the abyss made of the pain and miseries of thousands of people. He almost couldn’t free himself from the dark emotions.

“That’s too horrible!”

Li Yao tried his best to retract himself from the ocean of feelings. Looking at his dimming soul, he was still more or less frightened.

He finally understood why human beings chose to communicate with languages on the path of evolution when telepathy was so efficient and useful.

It was exactly because the interaction efficiency of telepathy was too high; so high that one feeling could contaminate every individual in a group very quickly until the whole group only had one feeling and one way of thinking.

If one man cried, the whole group would cry, and if one man laughed, the whole group would laugh with him. The fury of a single person might spread throughout the group instantly and cause a civil war in the next second!

Such a social structure was definitely far away from any kind of civilization.

Only the collective creatures like the Houyi Clan or the machine civilizations that were absolutely rational could choose telepathy as their way of information interaction.

The human brain was the hardest fortress for the soul of a human to hide in. They could think calmly and independently without being affected by radiations or the feelings of other people. That was the foundation for different thinking patterns and emotional statuses. Only when they were different could they explore different types of future!

But for Li Yao at this moment, his soul was too far away from his shell.

He was like a soldier who had taken off his crystal suit while confronting a stormy barrage almost nakedly!

Even though his soul was extremely sturdy, he could hardly compete with the feelings of billions of people.

The fire of his soul was dying and shivering like a candle in a storm.

“What a terrifying status!”

Li Yao thought to himself, Now, I’m only resisting the influence of other people by condensing my soul, but as someone in the Divinity Branching Stage, I’m supposed to split my soul into thousands of parts, resonate with millions of people, and absorb their powers for my training. Wouldn’t I get schizophrenia if something goes wrong?

Li Yao finally understood why he got a general picture of the Divinity Branching Stage so easily after only secluding himself for seven days.

It seemed that the most difficult thing was not to break into the Divinity Branching Stage but to keep one’s own heart from being influenced by other people when one could split their soul into thousands of parts after they entered the Divinity Branching Stage.

Come to think of it, after Wuying Qi reached the Divinity Branching Stage, was he really unchanged?

Or rather, did the darkness in the hearts of thousands of schemers and villains gather together and shaped Blackstar the Great, a so-called savior?

If Li Yao were submerged in the feelings and will of other people and was led by their desires and worries, what would his soul turn out to be in the end?

Greatly alarmed, Li Yao further condensed his soul.

He could not crawl into a random guy’s head.

The brain was a fortress for the soul. Even the brain of an ordinary person was extremely solid. They would be their own king in that domain.

Li Yao might be strong, but if he were to enter a random guy’s head, he might never be able to get out, like how poor Ou Yezi died in someone else’s head.

“The adults, the vigorous men, and the aggressive soldiers… Their vitality field is very powerful. Their brainwaves bounce like the most colorful flames. If my soul approaches them, I’m likely to be influenced by them or even be swallowed by them.

“It will be barely possible for me to communicate with those people.

“The seniors, the children, the sick, the depressed, and the sleepers… Their vitality fields are dim, and their brainwaves are like peaceful rivers. They’re not very dangerous. I can try to inject my virtual nerves into their vitality fields and transmit simple information to them.

“Huh. Why does it feel like one of the ghost stories in the ancient Cultivators’ notes?

“According to the ancient Cultivators, men are less likely to be invaded by ghosts and evil creatures, and the soldiers who have been through bloody battles can scare off any foul things. On the other hand, the old people, the children, and the devastated people are more likely to see ghosts. So, it’s not superstition. It’s just that the latter’s vitality field is weaker, and it’s easier for outsiders to mess with their retina and their optical nerves!

“There’s also the story where one can appear in someone else’s dream and make a request. It’s for the same reason. When someone is sleeping, they’re least wary and defended, and it’s easy to simulate their brain cells and present information in their dream.

“It’s not entirely impossible for me to even possess someone now. If I choose someone who is weak and unstable, I can invade his brain, hijack some of his brain nerves, and use his body remotely. Of course, it will be very dangerous and exhausting for both of us. There’s no need to do something so stupid unless it’s absolutely necessary.

“All in all, the ancient people were unaware of the approaches of systematic research and only concluded the phenomena, but they’re quite similar to what I’ve seen today.”

Li Yao thought quickly and tried his new techniques.

He should’ve spent a month if not a year on familiarizing himself with the new techniques.

But there was no time for that. The center of the cosmos was on fire. How could he take his time studying the techniques?

Concentrating his soul into a long, narrow streak of light, Li Yao left the city and flew to the east coast of the continent in a tube of spiritual energy.

In the blink of an eye, he had reached the coastline.

Through the tube, Li Yao continued the journey and dived into the ocean. Very soon, an island appeared before him. It was exactly Zhuge Jinglun’s Third Psychic Research Institute.

Li Yao’s soul leaped from the tides and observed the asylum from the sky.

The vitality fields of the psychopaths were a hundred times more violent and creepy than those of the ordinary people. Li Yao couldn’t help but feel frightened.

Carefully circumventing the psychopaths, he marched further into the town of the Covenant Alliance people through the tube.

There was no difference between day and night deep down the island. The place was as bright as day under the illumination of lights even though the sky was dark outside. The thousand captives of the Covenant Alliance were still working like tireless machines and building their home with their natural instincts.

The vitality fields of the Covenant Alliance were completely different from other people.

The brains of the normal people were like flowers, vortexes, mazes, or kaleidoscopes, and the brains of the Covenant Alliance people were like dry wells, frozen lakes, and hard rocks, nothing more.

While Li Yao was too scared to enter the brains of the normal people, the brains of the Covenant Alliance people seemed to be too boring to be studied.

Even so, Li Yao identified the person who seemed to be the sunspot without any problem.

Under his perception with his vitality field, the sunspot looked even more out of the picture.

Even though he was as busy practicing as other soldiers of the Covenant Alliance were, he simply felt different for Li Yao.

There was no telling if it was because of Wuying Qi 3.0’s influence, but Li Yao found the sunspot very amiable, as if they were connected by blood!

Li Yao’s soul couldn’t help but drift toward the sunspot’s head.

The sunspot’s head was not wary or resistant at all. Instead, there was some sort of uncanny attraction, as if he were welcoming the guest!

Even so, Li Yao was not Wuying Qi.

He was civilized and polite.

“Knock, knock, knock.”

Li Yao released threads of spiritual energy and tied up some of the sunspot’s auditory nerves before he said politely, “Hello, may I come in?”

Chapter 2724: In The Name Of God!

“Wow!”

After Li Yao made a polite request and was ready to visit the sunspot’s brain, the sunspot, greatly taken aback by the sound, had a natural reaction, and a bolt of lightning was struck at Li Yao’s soul.

Boom!

Li Yao’s soul was immediately blown apart. He was kicked back by a foot of thunder and lightning.

“Ahhhhhhhhhhh!”

Li Yao cried miserably.

“Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!”

Everything around him changed fast, and Li Yao’s soul was hauled back by a strange force across the ocean and the continent through the shaft in the Gold Crystal Pyramid back into his shell on the silver throne!

“Hiss!”

Li Yao leaped from his silver throne, feeling that he had just been punched in the head a hundred times!

“What happened?”

The world was swirling before Li Yao’s eyes. He had a strong headache, and suspicious fluids were leaking out of his mouth and eyes. He looked at himself in a mirror, only to see that his eyes were bloodshot.

“Dad!”

Xiaoming and Wenwen hurried to support Li Yao with their mechanical arms. They had increased the dosage of nutrition drugs to help him calm down and treat his wounds.

“Just now, we detected that your brainwaves were unusually high. Did you just catch the target unprepared and make him scared?”

Xiaoming analyzed and said, “When human beings are scared, a protective mechanism will be automatically activated in their brains to increase the intensity of brainwave output to block the intruders. So, you must not be rude. You should be patient and take it slowly!”

“Who’s rude? I’ve always been gentle, alright?”

Li Yao spat a mouthful of bloodstained saliva and rolled up his sleeves, before he announced, “Let’s try it again!”

“Are you sure?”

Wenwen said worriedly, “Extracting your soul from your body is very exhausting. Do you not need a rest, Dad?”

“Countless people are dying in the war every second. I have no time to rest!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth. “Just bring it on!”

For the second time, he sat down on the silver throne, and his soul was blossoming like fireworks.

He was more familiar with the process this time, and his soul reached the sunspot in the Third Psychic Research Institute as quickly as lightning.

Vaguely, Li Yao sensed that the sunspot’s brain was not as tranquil as before but had feeble ripples because of the shock just now.

He had left his companions who were training solemnly and was sitting by himself in a corner.

The captives of the Covenant Alliance in this town were all bottom-level workers and soldiers. They did not have a commander. So, none of them knew what to do with him and simply carried on their tasks with their natural instincts. As a result, Li Yao had a chance to talk to the sunspot alone.

With the lesson he learned last time, Li Yao did not let his soul approach the sunspot too closely this time. Instead, he asked patiently from far away. “Don’t be afraid. I’m not malicious. I’m just a friend. Can I come in?”

The sunspot shivered, and his eyes obviously bulged.

But he wasn’t really capable of independent thinking. The genetic marks in his body were so powerful that he subconsciously nodded and opened up his mind.

Greatly relieved, Li Yao pressed his soul into the sunspot’s mind.

“Who are you?”

Sensing an uncanny, magnificent, and majestic power appearing in his brain, the sunspot murmured with the purest and greatest delight, “You’re God! You’re the omniscient and benevolent God!”

“No, I’m not God. There are no gods in this world.”

It was Li Yao’s principle that he would not win the approval of the sunspot through lies and deception. He insisted, “I’m a friend. I’m here to—”

Hardly had he finished his sentence when he sensed that a bolt of lightning was quickly gathered in the sunspot’s head.

“You’re not God, but you have entered my brain!”

Holding his head, the sunspot said determinedly, “Then you must be an extraterrestrial devil. You’re the evilest of all things! Get out of my head!”

Boom! Clap!

Struck by the lightning again, Li Yao was blown away for a second time and was pulled back into his own shell in the Gold Crystal Pyramid by Xiaoming and Wenwen in time.

“I can’t believe this!”

Infuriated, Li Yao held his eyes, which were as swollen as rotten peaches, and said, “What idiocy and superstition! He’s really been poisoned for too long!”

“Dad, why don’t you let it go? We can work on other approaches.”

Wenwen said concernedly, “If it goes on like this, your soul will really be shattered, and you can never return to your body again!”

“No!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth, and the brilliance in his eyes was almost burning through the thick black blood. “If I cannot awaken only one sunspot, how am I going to awaken the dignity and hope of millions of Covenant Alliance people as human beings? Let me try it again!”

For the third time, Li Yao’s soul arrived at the Third Psychic Research Institute in an invisible streak of light. Penetrating through the walls, he reached the head of the sunspot.

The sunspot was panting and seemed to be utterly confused. He was apparently shocked by Li Yao’s previous contacts with him. Li Yao estimated that he had never been more emotional in his life than he was today.

“What’s your problem?”

Taking full precautions, Li Yao sent a thought down below again. “We were having a nice conversation, and I was very polite. If you don’t want me to come in, I can totally stay outside. Why did you strike me with lightning the next second you invited me? Isn’t it bad?”

“Ah!”

The sunspot’s eyes bulged again. Li Yao did not need to read him to know what he meant. “Devils! Extraterrestrial devils!”

“I’m not an extraterrestrial devil. Do you not find me familiar and intimate, as if we’re greatly bound? Why would you feel that way if I’m an extraterrestrial devil?”

Li Yao said, “Of course, I’m not a god either. But I was very close to the god that you worship and obey absolutely, and eventually, your god became part of me and entrusted his heritages and his mission to me. Can you understand the complicated relationship between us?”

Li Yao estimated that the sunspot could not understand it at all with his lack of logic thinking abilities.

However, the sunspot tilted his head and thought for a long time, before he sat down and opened his mind to Li Yao.

“You are the almighty and all-knowing God, and I’m your humblest servant.”

The message was sent from the deepest part of the sunspot’s brain.

Li Yao took a deep breath. Knowing that superstition could not be removed overnight, he decided to try to melt with the sunspot first.

For the third time, his soul stepped into the sunspot’s brain prudently.

When his virtual nerves that were gathered by his soul were connected to the real nerves of the sunspot, Li Yao heard a feeble noise. Then, something amazing happened. The whole world became a hundred times clearer and more vivid. He could see and hear everything around him clearly through the eyes and ears of the sunspot. He could sense the moist air, the gentle breeze, the scorching man-made sun, and the rough soil below his bottom through the sunspot’s skin.

It felt that he had been given a new body!

If he was willing to, he could even significantly enhance this body and fight in it as if he had really possessed the sunspot.

Of course, it was very exhausting and dangerous, not to mention that it was in violation of Li Yao’s beliefs. It was unnecessary for him to try it yet.

“No wonder Wuying Qi won so many battles in the war against the Covenant Alliance at the end of the Star Ocean Republic a thousand years ago! His military achievements made him one of the most distinguished persons in the country!”

Li Yao said, “It was because he had such a batch of excellent spies. He could predict what the Covenant Alliance’s army was going to do through the eyes and ears within the army. Of course he couldn’t fail a battle!”

After realizing that, Li Yao couldn’t have been more disgusted.

Wuying Qi acted mysterious among his own people, exterminated their feelings and will, and transformed them into his slaves after he reached the new level, pretending to be a god of those slaves, but what kind of unworthy god was that?

If the gods did exist, they would be the experts who led their own kind against external enemies and explored the vast universe so that everybody in their civilization would leap to higher levels!

Chapter 2725: Tough Drugs For Tough Men!

The sunspot’s soul was hiding at a corner in his brain.

This was his palace, but he couldn’t have looked more panicked and humble.

They were not conducting a verbal communication but a telepathic one. Thousands of words were exchanged within one instant.

“Almighty God, what can your humblest slave do for you?”

The sunspot’s soul flickered, expressing that he was willing to sacrifice himself for Li Yao.

Li Yao was even angrier, not because of the guy’s humbleness, but because he was incapable of waking the guy up immediately.

“I don’t need you to do anything for me. You only need to awaken your real identity, remember who you are, and reclaim your feelings and will. You are a human!”

Li Yao’s soul that was stretched out in the sunspot’s brain was displaying countless ever-changing pictures that depicted the life and the battles of the mortals in the Imperium and the federation.

In the pictures, people studied, worked, and lived. They were happy, angry, or sad. They sang, danced, and drank. Their songs gathered into colorful rivers that ran in the sunspot’s head that was almost entirely empty.

“Listen, I do not want to etch those pictures and sounds to your brain, because even if I do, they will still be fake. Their lives are not yours!”

Li Yao said, “But I really hope that you can give yourself an opportunity to appreciate the lives of the people and how splendid this world is without gods. Then, find your identity and live your life! I do not want to interfere with your choice, but I do hope that you have the right to choose. Every human being should have the right to choose their fate. That’s why we’re human beings. Do you understand me?”

The glamorous pictures and Li Yao’s deafening voices surged in the sunspot’s head, but they soon dripped off without leaving any mark in it.

The sunspot was silent.

Obviously, with his wisdom and modes of thinking, it was impossible for him to comprehend the pictures or “the right to choose”.

“I am Fang Gang 123, your humblest and most obedient servant.”

After a long silence, the sunspot sent a message.

Fang Gang 123 was a typical name in the Covenant Alliance.

Every child’s surname in the Covenant Alliance was determined according to the group that their parents belonged to when they were born. Then, their given name was randomly selected from a list of around thirty words. If the full name had been used, a number would be added to distinguish the name.

It was more like the number for a machine than a name for a human being.

If Li Yao had hair at this point, he would’ve torn off all of his hair and bashed his chest in fury.

Right then, someone vaguely chuckled on his back.

“Are you kidding me? Is this your plan to awaken the people of the Covenant Alliance?”

Li Yao was greatly taken aback by the mental devil’s voice.

“What the hell? Don’t sneak up on me like that, please!”

Li Yao said, “I haven’t sensed you since I secluded myself in the Gold Crystal Pyramid. You didn’t respond to me at all when I called you. I thought that I had already cleansed you, but I knew that you wouldn’t die so easily! So, please say something before you come out. I’m having goosebumps all over my body now that you suddenly decided to show up, like I just ran into a ghost!”

“Technically speaking, we are indeed a pair of twisted ghosts. You are as much a ghost as I am.”

The mental devil said casually, “The Divinity Branching Stage is of greater significance to me than it is to you, so I slept for another two days and received much more mysterious and unbelievable stuff while in deep meditation. It’s much more complicated than showing a few butterflies…”

“Oh? Is that so?”

Li Yao’s soul was suddenly frozen, before he exclaimed in utmost shock, “Wait, why… Why has your soul turned so handsome? That’s unreasonable! We’re the same person and share the same face, but why do you look so awesome when you’re in charge?”

The mental devil was still wearing Li Yao’s face, but its vibe had indeed greatly changed.

It was still made of bloody redness, but the redness was no longer monotonous but of different layers, as if it were made of thousands of different shades of red. It was most splendid.

Its long hair was moving both like surging tides of blood and burning fire. Together with its deep, bottomless eyes, it looked unpredictable and immeasurable.

The bloody robe that it was wearing made it look like a bloody lotus that was blossoming. The mental devil seemed both sacred and extremely evil.

Li Yao was never fond of bragging about himself, but he had to admit that he was actually quite good-looking if he dressed himself up. After he dressed himself up, he was definitely a hot extraterrestrial devil!

“I’ve told you that I meditated for two more days and realized more mysteries of the Divinity Branching Stage. Naturally, I’m much more handsome than you.”

The mental devil smiled. “Besides, darkness is always more attractive than light, and evil is always more interesting than justice. Do you disagree?

“Let’s focus on right now. You want to awaken the self-awareness and the feelings in this Covenant Alliance person, but look at what you’re doing… Work, life, singing, and dancing? That’s too boring! Do you think he’s a three-year-old? How could he be woken up by things like this?

“You have to prescribe tough men with tough drugs. Those sunspots were born in the Covenant Alliance and have been brainwashed too many times in their growth. Normal stimulations won’t work on them. You should bring out the really good stuff for them.

“I think that the porn videos you played last time were a creative idea, and you should definitely try it again. Of course, you don’t have to paste Wuying Qi’s face on the videos anymore. The original videos will be good enough. Besides, you can also consider waking his most primitive desires, as well as the negative feelings like anger and pain, with an immersive illusion. It’s always easier to burst into fury than to calm down, isn’t it?”

“You’ve got to be kidding me!”

Li Yao subconsciously refused it.

“Extraterrestrial devil!”

The sunspot named Fang Gang 123 realized what was going on too.

The Covenant Alliance was almost naturally vigilant of the extraterrestrial devils, and the mental devil at this point was definitely a great devil from every perspective.

Without any hesitation, Fang Gang 123 unleashed the most powerful lightning at the mental devil.

The mental devil smiled and snapped his fingers. Immediately, the lightning was smashed into smithereens, and Fang Gang 123’s soul was flung away.

“Do you see? This is the correct countermeasure. Your performance just now was humiliating.”

The mental devil snorted at Li Yao, and it drifted toward Fang Gang 123’s soul in its bloody robe. It smiled and said, “Little guy, don’t be anxious. You’re just an ignorant virgin, so you don’t understand what’s good about the extraterrestrial devils. By the time Uncle Li Yao etched the thousands of porn videos in his head into your brain, you will know what a happy life a man can enjoy! By then, I’m afraid that you will beg to become a normal person instead of remaining a natural eunuch in the Covenant Alliance. Hahahaha!”

“Hold on a second!”

Almost going crazy, Li Yao pulled the mental devil back with his soul tentacles. “What are you doing? If we can etch any random information in people’s brains, will we be any different from Wuying Qi or the Pangu Clan who regards human beings as slaves and machines?”

“Why do you always insist on meaningless moral principles?”

The mental devil frowned and said, “We’re only trying to cure them and awaken their instincts as a normal human being. Of course we’re different from Wuying Qi and the Pangu Clan. It’s a treatment!”

“I don’t want to be so stubborn either, but unfortunately, all of my non-stubborn traits have been absorbed and swallowed by you!”

Determined to maintain his principles, Li Yao clutched the mental devil. “Besides, too much is as bad as too little. Those Covenant Alliance people are essentially innocent kids and blank paper, but you want to portray dirty stuff on them? Do you have any idea what kind of monsters you will shape them into?

“Yes, it’s possible that they will get rid of their indifference and heartlessness, but it’s also possible that they will become a horde of monsters and murderers who are only interested in satisfying their desires… Wait, that’s your real purpose, isn’t it? You’re trying to walk from one extreme to the other. You want to transform the Covenant Alliance people into devils!”

“Oops!”

The mental devil stuck out its tongue and raised its hands before it grimaced and said, “What a loser I am! You saw through me again!”

Chapter 2726: The Brand-New Sunspot Plan!

“You—”

Li Yao was so angry that he almost burst into a fight with the mental devil. Gritting his nonexistent teeth, he held back his anger and said, “Let’s fight when we’re home, alright? We’re in someone else’s head right now.”

“Who’s fighting with you?”

The mental devil sneered and said, “Although my suggestion is partly based on selfish reasons, it’s also partly based on truth. Just look at his brain.”

The mental devil snapped its fingers, and a spark floated out to the center of Fang Gang 123’s brain before it exploded and illuminated the entirety of the brain.

Li Yao was immediately shocked.

If the brains of normal human beings could be compared to delicate, splendid palaces, Fang Gang 123’s brain would be a dilapidated building that had just been bombarded.

Not only had his sulcus been evened out, making his cerebral cortex as smooth as a mirror, but the nerves that connected different units of his brain were also broken and entangled and had clear marks of corrosion and burns..

Between the cerebral cortex, the limbic system, and the core, high walls had been established with thorny, abnormal neural synapses that were releasing electric arcs to suppress normal bioelectricity.

Because of the injuries, many parts in Fang Gang 123’s brain had shrunk significantly, and some other parts had expanded abnormally, occupying the living space of the normal brain cells.

No wonder he showed absolutely no response however Li Yao stimulated him.

It was because his brain had been brutalized!

“Do you see? All the sunspots were born and raised in the Covenant Alliance. Only in such a way could they carry the genes and soul marks that Wuying Qi gave the sunspots at the beginning.”

The mental devil said coldly, “It means that their brains have been reshaped by the leadership of the Covenant Alliance since they were little, until they’re utterly deformed.

“Just like what Zhuge Jinglun said, the connection between their cerebral cortex and their limbic system is cut off, the areas that are responsible for memories and feelings have severely shrunk, but the area responsible for instincts has abnormally expanded.

“This is a warrior of the Fire group. If my guess is correct, the Ultimate Benevolence Masters must’ve implanted tremendous knowledge into the area of natural instincts in his body, creating some sort of… program.

“When he is involved in a battle, the program will be activated like natural instincts, turning him into an efficient and heartless slaughter machine.

“However, in peacetime, he’s not even as wise as a three-year-old. He’s a complete retard.

“How are you going to awaken such a retard’s nonexistent self-awareness? Whatever you instill into his head, it will mean nothing for him at all!

“So, only my way can work. Imprinting the most vivid and stimulating information in his brain will likely raise his feelings and desires. Even dark feelings and desires are better than no feelings and desires, don’t you think so?”

“… Of course not!”

Li Yao’s soul constricted like a clenched fist. He roared, “Look at his brain! It’s essentially a burnt, desolate land where the soil has been contaminated with acids and magma.

“Your approach is to transplant some random trees from other places to this corrupt wasteland. Even though it will look green, it will be nothing but a lie!

“It’s not going to work! You can’t really wake him up by doing that. You will only turn him from the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ slave to your slave and the slave of desires.

“Do you not feel that what we really should be doing is to improve the quality of the soil by clearing all the acids and magma, sow tiny seeds, and slowly grow plants that belong to this soil so that his whole brain will be rejuvenated?”

Tilting its head, the mental devil thought for a while.

“Not exactly.”

It mocked, “How long is it going to take? What’s the price? What can you get from it?”

Li Yao stared at the mental devil in silence for a long time. Ignoring it, he flew to Fang Gang 123’s shivering soul.

“Don’t be scared. We’re friends. I’m your kind. I’m here to save you.”

Li Yao murmured and unleashed gentle light from his soul that enshrouded Fang Gang 123’s damaged nerves and withered brain cells.

Narrowing its eyes, the mental devil observed for a long time and finally realized what Li Yao was doing. It frowned again.

“You’re crazy.”

The mental devil sighed. “You’re helping this guy repair his brain at the cost of your precious soul power?

“It’s true that we’re both experts of brain recovery. Our brains exploded a couple of times every day when we trained and fought crazily in the past, but we barely asked for the help of Meditation Healers or brain surgeons. We simply connected the veins and cerebral nerves on our own. Our understanding of the brain is much better than most Meditation Healers and brain surgeons. We’re the top experts.

“However, you must bear in mind that this is not our brains, but the brain of someone hundreds of kilometers away from us. It’s ten times more difficult to fix than it is to fix our own!”

“Cut the crap.”

Focusing his attention, Li Yao split thousands of tentacles from his soul and helped Fang Gang 123 connect the broken veins and nerves and remove the redundancies that blocked the sulcus. He said to the mental devil without looking back, “You know that you can’t convince me.”

“But I can stop you!”

The mental devil gnashed its teeth. “Our souls are entangled and of the same origin. While you’re busy exhausting yourself, I’m being affected too! Idiot, I’ve already sensed that my power is fleeing! Stop now!”

“Stop me?”

Li Yao finally looked back at the mental devil. “Why don’t you try it?”

The mental devil shuddered and was too scared to say anything. After a long time, it simply snorted and sat down at the edge of Fang Gang 123’s brain where it sulked.

“What’s the point?”

Observing Li Yao coldly for a long time, it finally said, “This is just a bottom-level soldier of the Covenant Alliance and a common ‘bee’. Even if you can fix his brain and awaken his self-awareness, so what? Can it change the Covenant Alliance or save the federation and the Imperium? What’s the point?”

“Maybe it can’t.”

Still in deep concentration, Li Yao had helped Fang Gang 123 connect 3,425 nerves that were of paramount importance, which was about one-tenth of the whole task. He paused for a moment and said, “But it’s meaningful for him and myself.”

“… I still think that you’re adding to your trouble.”

The mental devil said again, “Don’t forget the details of the Sunspot Plan. Those sunspots are supposed to be the best tools. As long as you admit that you’re their god, they will tell you everything they know and even die for you voluntarily. If you gather enough sunspots, they won’t hesitate even if you ask them to attack the Ultimate Benevolence Masters.

“But you’re trying to awaken their humanity and self-awareness, turning them into real humans? What exactly do you want?

“Every human has weaknesses. They’re prone to fear, worries, and anger. There will be too many unexpected factors, and they won’t be devoted to you. Aren’t you making obstacles for yourself?”

“Listen up.”

This time, Li Yao did not look back, but he sounded more solemn than ever. “My Sunspot Plan is different from Wuying Qi 3.0’s Sunspot Plan.

“In his Sunspot Plan, all the sunspots were tools for him to fulfill his personal purposes and realize his ambitions. He did not care how many of the sunspots would die in the end.

“But my Sunspot Plan is different. I cannot regard a living human as a tool like Wuying Qi 3.0 did, even if it’s for a seemingly sacred and glorious purpose!

“I regard those sunspots as my own kind, my friends, and my comrades who fight by my side. If they’re fighting for something, I must make sure that they know it’s what they want!”

After a brief silence, the mental devil said gloomily, “You’re simply asking for trouble and increasing the difficulty of this mission.”

“Maybe, but you know that there are no shortcuts, and the methods which seem simple may lead to unexpected repercussions.”

Li Yao said, “Do you really think that Wuying Qi 3.0 sincerely tried to help us when he entrusted the Sunspot Plan to us?

“Idiot. The Sunspot Plan is his bait and the first step to allure us to become vicious dragons. If we carry out the Sunspot Plan according to his idea, regarding all the sunspots as pure tools and ignoring the dignity and free will of mankind, will we be any different from him? How can we claim that we’re righteous and he’s not?

“If sunspots can be regarded as tools for a lofty and urgent purpose, can all the people of the Covenant Alliance be regarded as tools for a purpose that is even more lofty and urgent? What about the people of the Imperium and the federation? Do you get it? The boundaries are right behind us. If we take one step back, we will totally collapse and have to walk down on Wuying Qi 3.0’s path.

“I will not let his scheme work out. My path is the path of thorns and fire, but I will see to the end of it!”

Chapter 2727: The Rules Of Swinging

When Li Yao’s soul returned to his shell at the bottom of the Gold Crystal Pyramid, he never felt more exhausted as if he just surfaced in an ocean from ten thousand meters deep.

The mental devil was right. Repairing someone else’s brain remotely and repairing his own brain was completely different.

It was like the difference between heaving an object that weighed a kilogram and heaving an object of the same weight a thousand kilometers away through remote manipulation.

Li Yao hadn’t entirely fixed Fang Gang 123’s brain yet. The man had been through so many surgeries since childhood that his brain palace was shaking vulnerably, and Li Yao had to dedicate a hundred times more attention and spiritual energy to the recovery.

But it was a good start anyway.

At least, toward the end of the recovery, when Fang Gang 123 heard a jolly song, ripples of delight spread out of his soul, like the purest smile of a child.

For Li Yao, such a smile was enough to offset all his pain and fatigue..

Xiaoming and Wenwen had been monitoring Li Yao closely and recording the data of Li Yao’s soul movements, partly to ensure Li Yao’s safety and health and partly to simulate Li Yao’s control and treatment with crystal processors. Their ultimate purpose was to establish an array of super crystal processors that could wake all the sunspots in the Covenant Alliance in Li Yao’s replacement.

Otherwise, it would’ve been impossible for Li Yao to save a lot of people if he were to wake them up one by one on his own.

“Dad, you have to take a rest.”

Xiaoming cut off Li Yao’s connection to the brainwave magnifier before he released spiritual waves of special frequencies with artificial limbs to help Li Yao massage his head and fix his soul. “Your soul has been away for six hours, and your brain is as terrible as if you’ve fought the most intense battle for six hours. Your soul might disperse if it stays outside for one more second!”

“Yes, take a good rest. You need to sleep deeply for another three hours before you continue the delicate surgery.”

Wenwen also said, “It’s both for your own good and for the sunspot’s sake. You wouldn’t want to connect his nerves or veins wrongly, would you?”

The reason convinced Li Yao.

Like a collapsed machine, Li Yao lay down and stretched out his arms, before he moaned in satisfaction.

“We’re off to a great start on the experiments of a soul leaving the body.”

Xiaoming continued, “We’ve collected enough data to set up the ground rule, which is that it consumes great mental power and spiritual energy for the soul to leave the body, and the brains of other people are extremely complicated and full of unexpected situations. Even the souls of the ordinary people or the low-level warriors may be able to hurt the intruders. It’s like some sort of rejection reaction.

“If the target’s brainwave frequency is similar to ours, or they’re the specially-modified products like the sunspots, the rejection reaction will be minimized, but it doesn’t mean that there will be no danger.

“So, we have to be more careful.

“Alright, Dad. Take a good rest. Is there anything else that you need?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao’s eyes closed, and he made the first snore, but his left eye suddenly opened weirdly in the color of blood. He smiled and said, “Neltharion is too small for me right now. Can you help me build a new body, preferably with super crystal processors as its head like yours? I can’t stand sharing the body with this opinionated moron. I need to get away from him as far as possible!”

“Huh?”

Xiaoming and Wenwen said simultaneously, “It’s you!”

“If you want a new body, maybe you should talk to the Fist King. He’s battle-type and is more suitable than you.”

Xiaoming thought for a moment and said, “Master Fist King is designing a new-generation Great Iron City. He’ll turn a space fortress into his body and can transform into a monolith dozens of times bigger than a Colossus. Are you interested in that?”

“That sounds rather awesome!”

The mental devil’s left eye glittered. “Of course I am! Ask him to build one for me too!”

“However…”

Wenwen asked, “Even if you can get into a new body, are you sure you can get away from Dad?”

The mental devil’s smile was gone.

“Fine, you’re right. There’s no absolute kindness, just like there’s no absolute evil. Even if I’m ten thousand lightyears away from that idiot, his stubborn, ridiculous, and hilarious kindness will still catch up to me, haunt me, and nag inside my soul nonstop like bloody quantum entanglement.”

The mental devil gnashed its teeth helplessly. “I can’t be separated from him. It was just a joke. How I hate it!”

The mental devil closed its eyes and cursed for a while. Half a minute later, it fell into the deepest sleep too.

…

Since that day, Li Yao had barely left the Gold Crystal Pyramid. He was completely devoted to the final tests of the Sunspot Plan.

Coincidentally, while he was simply trying to heal Fang Gang 123’s brain injuries, he discovered during his treatment that it was an effective way of training that could help him adapt to the new status where his soul was away from his body.

As Fang Gang 123’s brain was getting better and better, Li Yao’s soul power and his abilities to control his soul were soaring too.
