On one hand, it helps them to be more devoted to the gods, but on the other hand, they’re so innocent that they might be allured and corrupted by the extraterrestrial devils without them realizing it.

“The Purger is a defense line between the ordinary Covenant Alliance people and the extraterrestrial devils. They’re the guardians of the majority!”

Chapter 2740: : Something Is Wrong!

“It sounds that you’re quite great!”

Li Yao asked, “Can you tell me what exactly have you learned in the Holy Light Academy?”

“We learn how to identify and deal with dangerous items.”

Tang Ka said, “For example, when faced with colorfully-decorated and temptingly-delicious food, we have to remove their decorations and their flavors so that only their nutritions will be kept when we eat them.

“As for toys, clothes, and makeup that are not of any actual use, they will be burned immediately.

“The so-called ‘artworks’ are the most dangerous things of all. Our mentors told us that art represents mankind’s admiration for extraterrestrial devils and is the reflection of chaos in human beings’ subconsciousness. We were told to destroy artworks as soon as we spot them.

“Of course, there are other things that can be very valuable, such as the magic equipment and jade chips used in Imperium. Those items can be given to our warriors after the evil information in them is wiped out.”

.

“Interesting.”

Li Yao asked, “Anything more?”

“Besides those, we also learn about the complicated relations in the Imperium, such as families as well as the big or small interest groups based on those relations.”

Tang Ka said, “Our mentors told us that the Imperium people attach great importance to the meaningless concepts like bloodlines or family honor. They’re too insistent in passing on their genes without consideration of the fatal flaws in their genes, which results in the maldistribution of resources, social ossification, and family feuds. A lot of precious resources have been wasted because of all these internal conflicts even though the Imperium occupies the most prosperous worlds in the universe. After a thousand years, their civilization is not soaring but sinking. Grandpa Yao, is it true? Do the Imperium people really squander precious resources on unsuitable people just because ‘they’re my children’?”

“Well…”

Li Yao felt like touching his nonexistent nose. “You’re not wrong, but it’s too complicated to be explained for now.”

“Furthermore, we learn about the social classes of the Imperium of True Human Beings.”

Tang Ka said, “Our mentors told us that the Imperium of True Human Beings is the smelliest and evilest country where people care about nothing but desires. It’s a society where men eat men. The powerful people enjoy the most extravagant lives, whereas the weaklings can only live on the leftovers that the powerful ones accidentally drop without any hope of changing their lives.

“To make things worse, the powerful ones are even entitled to manipulate the weaklings. They’ve stolen the power of the gods and regarded the weaklings as their slaves. Is it true?”

“Well, now that you put it that way, the Imperium of True Human Beings feels like a country of devils to me too!”

Li Yao said, “However, speaking of which, do the powerful ones not take advantage of the weak ones in the Alliance of the Holy Covenant? Didn’t you just say that you were allocated to different tiers of classes, and many people were injured or killed when they awakened their talents? You’re qualified to keep your thinking and emotional abilities, but most of the workers and soldiers are not. Isn’t that exploitation and oppression too?”

“How can that be the same?”

After a brief stun, Tang Ka said, “It’s true that there are different classes in the Covenant Alliance, but they are just jobs in different regards, and none of them is nobler than the others. The soldiers and workers at the bottom level, the special classes like Purgers and the Lurkers, and the leaders like the Ultimate Benevolence Masters are all the gods’ most beloved lambs, most loyal servants, and most valiant warriors. We’re all the same!

“In order to build a more wonderful world with limited resources, it’s true that many people are working in harsh environments in the Covenant Alliance, but the products of their work are all dedicated to the improvement of the natural environment and the restoration of the Pangu civilization. None of their products is used to satisfy the personal desires of our leaders. By the way, none of us really have personal desires!

“In the evil Imperium of True Human Beings, when the miners struggle to make a living in the cold and dark mines, the owners of those mines are wasting resources unconcernedly in the most splendid buildings.

“But in the Alliance of the Holy Covenant, when the miners are busy at work, the special classes, the commanders, and the Ultimate Benevolence Masters are suffering the same way and living the same holy and abstinent life, devoted to the most sacred cause.

“I’ve never seen the life of the Ultimate Benevolence Masters in person, but our mentors live and eat with us. Our food tastes the same, and the mentors do not have any so-called entertainment, certainly not the evil pleasure of playing with the bodies of the weak. It’s definitely not going to happen!

“As for the test to awaken talents, the casualty rate is indeed quite high, but it’s fair for everyone. Our mentors and director went through the same thing years back.

“More importantly, there are no privileges as every test is absolutely open and equal. It doesn’t matter who your parents are. As long as you’re talented, you will be sent to the most suitable post to serve the gods. If you’re not, you will be purged in the holy temple to be a hard and holy cornerstone of our country.

“I was told that things are different in the Imperium. In the Imperium, the offspring of the powerful may be promoted to important posts even though they have no corresponding talents, and the offspring of the weak, even though blessed with unparalleled talents, are very likely to remain undistinguished and even trodden upon. Is that really so, Grandpa Yao?”

“I find it impossible to answer your question.”

Li Yao said, “Maybe, but a reform is going on in the Imperium of True Human Beings. I believe that many things will change…”

“Change?”

Tang Ka asked, “Will it turn into a country without oppression, exploitation, and inequality like the Alliance of the Holy Covenant?”

“I think that’s too challenging.”

Li Yao said, “Hey, my young friend, your requirement is too high!”

“Nothing is going to really change as long as selfishness still remains.”

Tang Ka reached a conclusion. “It seems that the evilness and shortcomings of the Imperium of True Human Beings that my mentors described are all true. I didn’t believe that there was such a horrible country and such an absurd system at all. In comparison, the Covenant Alliance is perfect!”

“Wait, my mind is quite messy at this moment. Let me calm down first.”

Li Yao said, “It sounds that your courses are truly complicated.”

“They’re indeed very hard, particularly those about feelings.”

Tang Ka said, “We can’t be too inert in sensing feelings. If we’re too inert, we wouldn’t be able to comprehend the dangerous implications of a lot of items from the Imperium. You know that most of the time the traps of extraterrestrial devils are not on the surface but hidden between the lines. We must detect them keenly.

“However, we can’t be too keen either. If we’re too keen, we’re likely to be influenced by the extraterrestrial devils and end up as their slaves.

“All in all, only one in a hundred students in the Holy Light Academy can make it to the ultimate test. The rest will have to be engaged in simpler jobs after being modified in the holy temples.

“There are seven… no, six days left. As long as I pass the test, I’ll become a real Purger. So, I don’t want anything to happen to me or my monitor. I only hope that we can successfully become Purgers. Grandpa Yao, please help us!”

Li Yao was silent for a long time. His thoughts were flying and colliding.

“Listen, my young friend, I’ll certainly help you. However, after carefully analyzing what you just said and comparing it to what I know, I’ve noticed quite a few things that are not right.”

Li Yao said cautiously, “First of all, I can tell that the cerebral activities of you and your classmates are too powerful. Really. In my senses, your brains are like colorful torches, which are completely not like the coldness and lackluster of the Covenant Alliance people I met before.

“Maybe you’ll argue that it’s a feature of the Purgers, but I still think that something is wrong. Such Purgers are too dangerous.

“Secondly, I’ve got in touch with some of the Lurkers before. I’m aware of the brain structures and the soul features of the Lurkers. The Lurkers do not suppress their feelings; they simulate their feelings. They don’t have to suppress their feelings because they don’t have feelings at all.

“I think that if I were an Ultimate Benevolence Master, I could’ve trained all the Purgers in exactly the same way as the Lurkers.

“Thirdly, I’m here. My arrival suggests that something will happen, because every time I showed up, accidents, schemes, lies, betrayals, disasters, chaos, and destruction would follow. Do you think it’s going to be an exception this time?”

Tang Ka was dumbfounded. “… What?!”

Chapter 2741: Training At Night!

“It’s nothing. Just chill. All in all, you only need to trust me like you trust God Pangu. I’ll take care of everything.”

Li Yao said, “Now, do you recall anything weird that happened recently?”

“Anything weird?”

Tang Ka thought for a moment and mumbled, “I think so!”

“Oh? Really?”

Li Yao grew excited. “What is it? Please tell me!”

“During the day, my monitor, who had been cold and pious, suddenly dragged me to an empty supermarket and took off her clothes, before she told me that she believed that the Ultimate Benevolence Masters were wrong.”

Tang Ka said, “Then, I had this most absurd dream at night where my monitor was whipped in a red dress. When I was half asleep and half awake, some of my sticky life essence gushed out, and when I was overwhelmed, an extraterrestrial devil who called himself a good guy entered my head.. Are these things not weird?”

“… Fine, kid. I know that you’re been greatly shocked today. Otherwise, I couldn’t have entered into your head when your soul was weak and disordered.”

Li Yao could only make other plans. “Let’s just take our time. Whatever scheme is behind this ultimate test, you have to learn to control your feelings and hide my existence. You must prevent anyone from discovering any anomaly in you.

“I don’t want you to play a hero. Really, before my arrival, I didn’t know whom I would possess. If you were a rude and hideous man, I might feel less guilty and would make the best use of you without hesitation. But I don’t want to ruin the future of a fourteen-year-old young man who had his first wet dream only last night.

“Therefore, even if there is really any scheme, I wouldn’t force you to reveal it or change anything. It’s hard. It’s too hard. I only hope that you will not be changed by the world and that you can keep your thinking and your emotional abilities.

“Do you get it? If everything goes well, I’ll find a more suitable platform within three days and leave your body, but of course, I will give you a little something before my departure. However, we must be very prudent until then.

“Now, fully cover yourself with your quilt and raise your wrist crystal processor so that I can see it more clearly. Then, relax and imagine that you’re in a warm ocean where your veins and your cells are stretching out comfortably. Let me take control of everything…”

“What are we doing?”

Tang Ka covered himself and raised his wrist crystal processor before he asked curiously.

“Didn’t you say that a chip that can monitor your biophysical changes was embedded in this? Since your biophysical parameters are likely to change drastically in the coming days, maybe I can adjust your wrist crystal processor so that it would be less… sensitive,” Li Yao explained.

Then, his soul sprawled out and grew quickly like a vein that was quickly splitting apart in the young man’s body.

“This is—”

Tang Ka’s eyes popped out as he had never had such a feeling before. “I feel that ten thousand worms are slithering in my body. I’m so hot! No, I’m so cold! Ah, so itchy!”

“Forgive me, but it’s my first time too. I’m trying to control your nerve endings and your muscle fibers so that my soul can take over your body harmoniously.”

After a brief silence, Li Yao continued, “Would you please stop making those weird noises? Damn it. I really do not know which is more perverted now, to invade a man’s body or a woman’s!”

“I… I’ll try… Ah!”

Tang Ka couldn’t help but groan with disbelief beaming out of his eyes. He didn’t do anything, but his hands were moving agilely under the control of an uncanny power.

In his class, Tang Ka, as a member of Metal, was famous for the stability and fastness of his hands. It only took him half an hour to complete the maintenance work that his classmates would take hours to do.

However, even when he was in his best state, Tang Ka had never seen his fingers dancing so elegantly and accurately!

He felt that his fingers had turned into ten streaks of light that gathered into a silver ball of light. His eyes couldn’t keep up with his fingers at all, and he could only watch the gestures and the runes created by the light ball, which lingered for a long time in the air.

“This… This is too fast. It’s unbelievable! This is even more awesome than the work of the strongest Metal mentors in the Holy Light Academy! Are they really my hands?”

Tang Ka was shocked.

“This is way too slow. It seems that your body is slightly rejective of my soul, which significantly reduces my speed. It’s been a very long time since I maintained and modified anything at such a low speed. I almost forgot how it felt,” Li Yao complained.

“Huh?”

Tang Ka didn’t know what to say. “Are… Are you serious?”

“Of course I am!”

While talking, Li Yao tapped Tang Ka’s portable crystal processor with one finger. Or at least in Tang Ka’s eyes, he did nothing but a tap.

But then, his portable crystal processor fell apart without a sound!

Tang Ka’s eyes bulged. He was going to remind this mysterious and powerful Grandpa Yao that the alarm system on his crystal processor would automatically send a message to his mentors when it could not sense his breath or heartbeat due to external damage or system malfunctions when he noticed that the portable crystal processor that had been dismantled into hundreds of components was still running around his wrist under the boost of a gentle but sticky power!

It was the rarely-seen “hot maintenance” technique, which could change the component on a piece of magic equipment or upgrade it without disrupting it. When hot maintenance was done, the magic equipment’s performance would change greatly without being restarted.

A mentor in the academy had described it to the students, including Tang Ka, before. He said that it was an extremely rare technique that only the real blessed ones could possibly awaken.

Little did Tang Ka expect that he would see an extraterrestrial devil performing such a technique one day!

“It’s true that the Covenant Alliance is more advanced than the Imperium in terms of technologies in certain domains!”

While commanding Tang Ka’s right hand to move the hundreds of components, Li Yao studied them with great interest. “Very advanced privilege technologies have been adopted even though this is only a portable crystal processor for teenage students. If it were in the Imperium, probably only the officers above a certain rank would have the privilege to use such portable crystal processors.

“But is it also a proof that you’re much more dependent on the Spiritual Nexus and crystal processors than the Imperium people are? How interesting. Let me study this immaculate structure…”

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

A hundred structural designs suddenly appeared on Tang Ka’s retina. He read them, only to discover that they were all for his portable crystal processor.

Every design was vivid and consisted of the tiniest details.

“Let me see. Ah. Here’s the chip that monitors biophysical parameters. It’s not hard to block it. But as a result, you would be utterly unemotional, which would likely expose you too. So, it seems more reasonable to reduce its sensitiveness by 50%.”

Li Yao said, “Focus, kid. Imagine that your hands are melted in the air like ice melting in warm water. That’s right. Just like this…”

Tang Ka suddenly felt excruciating pain in his right hand.

It was not ice melting in warm water, but more like ice melting in magma.

He could even smell the scent of his skin and his flesh being burned. He was about to cry out in pain when the dismantled wrist crystal processor suddenly returned to his left arm in one piece in the blink of an eye!

Had it not for the agony on his right hand that was caused by high-speed frictions with the air, he would almost suspect that everything just now had been his illusion!

“Are… Are you done?”

Tang Ka asked, finding it hard to believe, “What kind of speed was that?”

“The speed of a snail. There’s nothing I can do. Your hands are too fragile. They aren’t like a pair of hands that belongs to a refiner at all. I fear that your fingers would’ve been broken if I were a little bit faster.”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, “I have a secret technique for hands training that I can teach you. I guarantee that you can do anything you want with your hands after you get the hang of this technique.

“However, first things first. I’ll teach you how to control your feelings and how to manage your breath, your heartbeat, and your micro-expressions, which tend to leak what you’re thinking.

“I think that even in a sh*thole like the Covenant Alliance, as long as you learn to hide your thoughts deeply, there’s always going to be a way for you to avoid the so-called ‘purging’ and keep your real self.

“Now, focus your attention, sense your brain, remember all the nerves and veins in your head, and imagine that cold water is flowing from the top of your head to every vein and every nerve in your body…”

Chapter 2742: Shadows Of The Ultimate Benevolence Masters

Under Li Yao’s teaching, Tang Ka spent the whole night practicing the tricks to control his heartbeat, his breath, and even his brainwaves on his bed.

Li Yao told him that the key point to hide his soul without exposing himself was to imagine that he was frozen magma that was still burning inside or a rock under a waterfall that was still hard even though all its edges had been worn away.

When the dawn came and horns were blown in the camp to wake the students hard, Tang Ka finally heard his heartbeat and the sound of his blood in the absolute darkness.

It was a very amazing feeling. It was like his shell and his thoughts were completely separated. Nobody how active and excited his mind was, his body could remain inexpressive and indifferent with absolutely the same heartbeat and breath.

Li Yao told Tang Ka that this was a trick for snipers and professional assassins. Those people had to reduce all their biophysical activities to the point that they looked like corpses so that they could hide themselves, but in the meantime, they had to consider every detail of their operations in their heads without any stop so that they could seize the crucial time window to kill their targets.

Li Yao was a great assassin himself. He knew that the trick would be very helpful for Tang Ka to disguise his real feelings and thoughts..

When Tang Ka opened his eyes, a new gate seemed to have been opened for him, and the world became different.

He sat up and observed his classmates nearby who were equally expressionless. A strange idea occurred to him. He wondered how many of his classmates really did not have feelings, and how many of them were only not showing their feelings although their feelings were surging in their heads like he did.

Was his monitor really as indifferent and devoted as she appeared?

No, the monitor must have her extraterrestrial devil too, just like his own.

He wondered what the monitor’s extraterrestrial devil looked like.

Suddenly, Tang Ka did not hate being possessed by an extraterrestrial devil anymore. It seemed rather fun.

Was it possible that everybody had an extraterrestrial devil in their heart, except that they never admitted it?

As whistles were blown, Tang Ka stopped thinking and started the tiresome morning exercises.

Body exercises for teenagers were important in the Covenant Alliance. Perhaps it was because they would barely have the energy to think if they were too exhausted. Even though Tang Ka and his classmates had purge operations for the day, their morning exercises were not canceled. However, Tang Ka was already used to it. He could even practice the technique to hide his feelings while he exercised his body!

After the morning exercises, before the sticky and tasteless breakfast was served, it was the second most important moment of the day that was only less important than the prayer at dusk.

All the students, the mentors, and even the soldiers of the purge troop nearby turned on their portable crystal processors and switched them to a special channel, and five different images were soon projected from their crystal processors.

The five images were peaceful and sacred old men who were wearing linen clothes and thorny belts.

Their eyes were pure black, like channels leading to the deepest part of the universe. No matter how the images were moved, they seemed to be staring at the holder of the crystal processor in the eyes and even seeing through their heart.

They were exactly the five Ultimate Benevolence Masters of the Covenant Alliance!

“Praise the gods for letting your beloved lambs see a new honor. May your glory and your power be sung over throughout the universe…”

Led by the Ultimate Benevolence Masters, Tang Ka, his classmates, and his mentors prayed quietly.

After the brief prayer, he briefed the Ultimate Benevolence Masters on what he did yesterday and asked the Ultimate Benevolence Masters if he were mentally steady enough to resist the extraterrestrial devils and carry on the strenuous and dangerous purge operation. After the Ultimate Benevolence Masters gave a positive reply, he recited another prayer and completed the morning ritual.

During the whole ritual, Li Yao had been lurking in the young man’s soul and did not ask about it.

He was not scared of being spotted by the Ultimate Benevolence Masters. But he was afraid that the young man would expose himself when he was distracted. After all, the young man had just learned the way to control his brainwaves and his biophysical parameters.

After everybody finished their morning prayer and buried their head into the buckets of paste that was served to them, Li Yao asked in a low voice, “Do you have to report what you did during the last day as well as what you’re going to do next to the Ultimate Benevolence Masters every morning?”

“Yes.”

Tang Ka devoured the paste, but he somehow recalled the lollipop that his monitor stuffed into his mouth the previous day. That lollipop was so sweet that he could not tolerate his regular food anymore.

Why had he never sensed that the food he had had for a dozen years was so awful? It almost tasted like… melted candles!

Struggling to swallow a mouthful of synthesized food, Tang Ka tried to keep his face unchanged while he spoke to Li Yao in his head.

“We do not often see the Ultimate Benevolence Masters in person, but the Ultimate Benevolence Masters care about us and guide our way every day.”

“I just noticed that around a thousand people were praying in the camp. If counting the soldiers nearby, the number will be even larger.”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and asked, “Also, all the people have to do the morning prayer in the Covenant Alliance, right? Then how do you determine the time for morning prayer? According to the rotation cycle of every specific planet, or is it held at a fixed time for all planets?”

“If we’re on habitable planets, considering our biological clock and our energy, we’ll have morning and dusk prayers according to the sunrise and sunrise of the specific planets.”

Tang Ka explained to Li Yao. “But if we’re on starships, we will no longer be restricted to the rotation cycle of a certain planet. Most starships have the same schedule.”

“That is to say, tens of billions of people may have the morning prayer at the same time.”

Li Yao analyzed, “But there are only five Ultimate Benevolence Masters, correct? However powerful their computation ability is, it’s impossible for them to process the prayers and reports of tens of billions of people simultaneously.

“However, I saw clearly just now that the same Ultimate Benevolence Master reacted differently to different people who were praying.

“So, it was not the Ultimate Benevolence Masters that received your prayer, but some sort of fake, simulated shadows. Is that correct?”

Tang Ka was stunned for a long time.

“I’ve never considered that question before.”

Tang Ka said, “The Ultimate Benevolence Masters are the First Apostles blessed by the gods. They were created by the gods’ last drop of blood in our world. Is it strange that they can listen to the prayers of billions of people at the same time?”

“Fine, let me ask in a different way.”

Li Yao asked without giving in, “Have you ever seen an Ultimate Benevolence Master in the flesh, who has to pee and poop like other people?”

Tang Ka was silent for a long time.

“No.”

He said, “Normally, we can only sense the Ultimate Benevolence Masters during the rituals in the morning and at dusk. Occasionally, the Ultimate Benevolence Masters will send holy light to one of us so that the person could speak for them. Sometimes, the Ultimate Benevolence Masters will also present enormous illusions in the sky to announce oracles.

“As for the Ultimate Benevolence Masters in the flesh… It’s true that I’ve never seen them, but that’s not strange. The Ultimate Benevolence Masters are busy and have to spend most of their time meditating and communicating with gods. Why would they waste their time on a Mr. Nobody like me?”

“Well, I don’t want to desecrate your Ultimate Benevolence Masters and your gods, so I can’t say that their tricks are fake.”

Pausing for a moment, Li Yao continued, “But let’s say I can control the Spiritual Nexus of this whole planet and I have a weather troop that is good at creating astounding weather phenomena in the sky, I would be able to pretend to be an Ultimate Benevolence Master, wouldn’t I?”

Tang Ka frowned again.

But thankfully, he was covered by the paste bucket, and he noticed his anomaly instantly.

“That is blasphemy!”

Tang Ka said, “They’re the Ultimate Benevolence Masters!”

“Okay, fine. I was wrong. I definitely do not plan to impersonate an Ultimate Benevolence Master. I’m only discussing the technological feasibility with you.”

Li Yao changed the topic and asked, “Tell me more about your mentors. Do they and the director of your academy have meals with you?”

“No, the director has the meals with the commander of the troop nearby, but our food is the same.”

Tang Ka pointed his mentors out to Li Yao.

Pitifully, none of those mentors was a sunspot. At the very least, Li Yao did not sense the amiable air in them as he did from Tang Ka.

Some of the students did attract Li Yao’s attention, but since they were students just like Tang Ka, it was pointless for Li Yao to swing to them.

A swing could be very exhausting and dangerous. Li Yao did not want to make any reckless move unless it was a very suitable target.

Chapter 2743: The Lair Of Extraterritorial Devils!

The breakfast was over ten minutes later, and the teenagers had gathered in classes again after putting on the suits that could allegedly resist germs, viruses, and extraterritorial devils, ready to reenter the battered town that was haunted by extraterritorial devils.

It was also the first time that Li Yao saw the purge troop nearby through Tang Ka’s eyes. He saw teams of fully-armed soldiers in crystal suits, supersonic shuttles in the sky, and floating fortresses that looked like black mountains. They were monitoring and keeping all the ruins under control.

In the meantime, Li Yao sensed that many of the thousand teenagers had rapidly increasing breathing patterns or heartbeats.

“I’m more and more positive that something is wrong with this ultimate test.”

Li Yao said casually, “Last night, you told me that the students, including you, were put into a real-life operation because there weren’t enough soldiers for that, which agrees with my intelligence. As far as I know, there are not many troops on this planet.

“However, when you already have insufficient soldiers, such a massive troop is still deployed next to you to do nothing but watch over you for seven days. Isn’t it too wasteful?

.

“Even though the commander of this purge troop is hoping to recruit new soldiers, there is no need to keep his whole troop here. A few officers should’ve been more than enough.

“So, the purge troop must be here for other purposes, say, to deal with potentially dangerous situations, right?”

Tang Ka looked at the station of the purge troop and said in confusion, “It’s impossible for the Imperium to take back the Thick Soil Sector any time soon. Even if they can, I don’t think there is anything valuable here that is worth the defense of such a huge troop!”

“That’s right. I don’t think the purge troop is defending against the Imperium’s army either. So, there can only be one answer.”

Li Yao said calmly, “They’re defending against you?”

“Us?”

Tang Ka found it hard to believe. “But why? We’re future Purgers! I dream to be one of them!”

“I’m afraid that’s the problem.”

Li Yao sighed and said, “As far as I know, few people in the Covenant Alliance ‘dream’, and the requirements for the Purgers will only be higher. How can they let someone who dreams to become a Purger?”

“But… But it’s the vocational requirement of a Purger!”

Tang Ka’s feelings surged like a tsunami again. “We must have certain feelings and thoughts, or the ability to dream, in order to identify dangerous items!”

“No, you’re wrong. With detailed lists and descriptions on dangerous items, 99% of them can be recognized by programs.”

Li Yao said coldly, “The Purgers do not need to know what ‘art’ is at all. All they need to do is to destroy it when they see a colorful painting. Wouldn’t that be convenient and reliable? Even though some Purgers who have thinking abilities are needed, not everybody has to have the ability to think.”

“What are you getting at?”

Tang Ka mumbled, “What do you mean? What’s this if it’s neither our graduation test nor the recruitment of the purge troop?”

“I don’t know. I need more information for further analysis.”

Li Yao said, “I only know that the thousand kids here mostly boast fiery feelings and lightning-like thoughts just like you do. Even though I’m hiding in you, I can tell that they’re different from the regular people of the Covenant Alliance. The ice in their heads is cracking.

“It’s actually understandable. You’ve been living on boring starships for a decade faced with nothing but walls. Your desires and your thoughts are pressed down to the limits like a spring.

“But all of a sudden, you’re dropped to a habitable planet faced with a… rather resourceful town of the Imperium. Although this town has been ruined by war, the remaining resources in this town are still enough for you to unleash your nature and have fun for seven days and seven nights.

“For the seven days, you can do anything you want in this city, and all the adults simply observe you from a distance away. What’s this? It’s like throwing a man who has been hungry for three days to a buffet restaurant where he can eat anything he wants!

“If this is the way to test the Purgers, I have to say that it’s too wasteful and complicated.”

Tang Ka was silent for a long time.

“I…”

He sobbed silently, “I want to be a Purger! That’s my dream!”

“Don’t be excited. The more excited you are, the further away you will be from your dream.”

Li Yao calmed the young man down. “The only good news is that you’re not the most excited and ‘abnormal’ kid of the thousand students here. Actually, after you practiced the trick I taught you, you appear quite calm. Other people are even worse than you!

“I thought that you were the only ‘weirdo’ who had abundant thoughts and feelings. However, it turns out that all of you are the same. It’s possible that the thousand students, including you, are screened out according to a certain standard. Or maybe…”

“Or maybe what?” asked Tang Ka.

“Nothing. Let’s continue and see what’s next.”

Li Yao was going to say that those kids who were too emotional to be the children of the Covenant Alliance’s people were probably “manufactured” according to a certain standard.

But then he decided not to say it out in case the young man was even more disturbed.

Led by their monitor, the teenagers in Tang Ka’s class came to the edge of the town in their mentors’ crystal tanks, before they entered the purge area on foot.

There was no telling if it was because of Li Yao’s psychological hint, but Tang Ka did feel that a lot of people were different today.

When they entered the city the previous day, they were like cold machines that were only carrying out the purge operation as instructed.

However, today, their eyes, their breath, their faces, and their gaits were all unnatural.

Tang Ka thought for a long time and realized why they were unnatural.

Just like him, they both looked forward to the town and dreaded it. Or rather, they dreaded themselves in their hearts.

Crossing the statue they broke the other day, they pressed forward more deeply and reached a three-floored building with a yard. Inside the yard were a carousel and a slide.

“This is…”

Tang Ka’s eyes were glued to everything he saw. “A kindergarten? The place where the Imperium people raise their offspring, like our nursery?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao said, “This is a ‘Palm Tree’. All the kids are supposed to cover their hands with colorful pigments before they press their hands on the wall. The marks they leave will be like the leaves of a tree.”

“… I don’t get it.”

Tang Ka said, “What’s the purpose of such lousy training?”

“Nothing.”

Li Yao said, “It’s only for fun.”

“They seemed to have had a lot of fun.”

Tang Ka focused his eyes on the wall in the empty kindergarten. Hundreds of paintings of the kids were stuck to the wall, depicting their happiest moments and their childish hope in the most glamorous colors and the worst techniques.

“Yes. They had every reason to be happy.”

Li Yao explained, “The first batch of colonizers who participated in the reconstruction of this planet had mostly been bankrupted back in their hometown. Their kids were not capable nor qualified to go to a kindergarten back there.

“But in this new paradise that was full of hope, their kids had a chance to live a happy life that they did not dare to imagine in the past. I think that’s why the colonizers named the city ‘New Joy’.

“However, those colonizers did not consider the risks of their option. Or maybe, they did not have any other options, and this was their only land of hope.

“Where are the kids in this kindergarten now?”

“I don’t know.”

Tang Ka suddenly lost the courage to look at the things around him, particularly the colorful tree of hope that the kids painted with their hands. “There were no massive engagements in the city. The kids should be fine. They must’ve been sent to the rear…”

“To be purged?” Li Yao asked.

For some reason, Tang Ka did not even have the guts to say yes. The word “purge”, filled with venomous magma, burned his soul.

Tang Ka and Li Yao fell silent. In their silence, they heard the heavy breathings and heartbeats of the teenagers, or big kids, near them.

“Level-one hazardous area!”

The monitor roared, “This is the lair of extraterritorial devils where they proliferate. Purge this place at once!”

Nobody moved. All the “Children of Holy Light” stood in silence and stared at everything around them, deep in thought or confused, wondering about the lifestyles of the other human beings.

“What are you waiting for? Did you not hear me?”

The monitor raised her voice and shrieked, “Purge! Purge! Purge everything in this place!”

“Ah!”

Liao Meng, the deputy monitor, roared, and sweat dripped from his pale face. More ruthless than ever, he raised his flamethrower and fired at the palm painting, the toys, and the carrousel of the kids.

“Ahhhhhhhhhhh!”

All the Children of Holy Light pulled the triggers and destroyed everything they could see. Very soon, the kindergarten was mired in an ocean of fire and smoke.

Chapter 2744: Destiny And Truth

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

Under the scorching fire, every teenager’s face looked particularly excited and ruthless, and their portable crystal processors were all shrieking, indicating that everybody except Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka had drastic biophysical changes. The crystal processor of Liao Meng, in particular, was even glittering in red; a sign that he was on the verge of loss of control.

It was not until three minutes later that the kids finally calmed down.

“Did I lose control?”

Looking at his shivering hands, Liao Meng mumbled in disbelief, “I lost control?”

The other kids dare not look at each other in the eyes, as if they were feeling guilty.

They also seemed to be tacitly keeping what happened just now a secret to themselves.

They had planned to set this place as a rendezvous point, but nobody was willing to stay next to the burnt building and carrousel. In the end, they walked on and set a plaza nearby as the rendezvous point.

.

Then, everybody fled from each other to confront themselves on a deep level at quiet corners.

“Monitor, do you not feel that everybody acted weirdly just now?”

Returning to the supermarket that they visited yesterday and locking the door, Tang Ka spoke to Chu Zhiyun eagerly.

Actually, he was desperate to share the uncanny things that happened to him since last night, including his sticky underwear, “Grandpa Yao” who appeared out of nowhere, and his feeling that could almost be called shame when he was faced with the toys and paintings left by the kids in the kindergarten.

The kindergarten in reality had been consumed by fire, but another kindergarten that was more splendid rose in his head and rooted in his every brain cell, making it impossible for him to get rid of it.

He remembered something he read a long time ago.

“I could’ve tolerated the darkness, if I had never seen light.”

Tang Ka felt some feelings that he could not quite describe were seething in his heart, like cracking ice and sprouting seeds, making it impossible for him to tolerate what was before his eyes, including the kindergarten just now and this dazzling supermarket.

He couldn’t stop himself from peeping at the delicious-looking foods, and an evil voice kept whispering to him, “It’s fine. You can take that. It’s fine. Everything in this supermarket belongs to you. Hahahaha…”

“Wait.”

Tang Ka said in his heart, “Grandpa Yao, it wasn’t you who were talking, was it?”

“Of course it was. It’s unnecessary for you to hold yourself back at all!”

Li Yao said, “If you want to eat them, just eat them. Scientists have pointed out that carbohydrates can help you stay delighted and focused. Really!”

Tang Ka: “…”

“I don’t care if they’re weird. I don’t plan to become a Purger anyway. In fact, the weirder they are, the better, because nobody would pay attention to us in such a case,” said Chu Zhiyun unconcernedly.

Much more spontaneous than Tang Ka, she had already picked up a bottle of candies and was enjoying them. She said, “All I have to do is to dig out my talents and prove the Ultimate Benevolence Masters wrong. Open your mouth!”

She put a piece of candy in Tang Ka’s mouth forcefully.

“Ah, no!”

Tang Ka blushed with a racing heart again. He stammered, “We… We can’t do this!”

“What a useless man. If a tiny candy is enough to defeat you—” Chu Zhiyun snorted. “Wouldn’t your loyalty to the gods be too weak? Don’t spit it out. Chew it and swallow it. If you spit it out, I’ll put ten more candies in your mouth!”

“I…”

Tang Ka’s face was even redder. He completely forgot Li Yao’s training at night and somehow recalled the picture of his bold monitor being tied up and whipped hard in her red dress again.

“Wait, just to be clear—”

Li Yao asked, “Is your wish to save the damsel in distress when your monitor is tied up or simply to watch her being tied up and whipped?”

“What are you talking about?”

Embarrassed, Tang Ka hesitated for a long time and said, “Monitor, why are you so dogged in pointing out the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ mistake?”

“Because—” Chu Zhiyun walked to the female clothes area on her tiptoes. “That’s why I exist! That’s gods’ mission for me!

“Think about it. The Ultimate Benevolence Masters are humans, and humans err. So, something has to point out the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ mistakes so that the mistakes won’t escalate and even collapse the Covenant Alliance, right? But have you heard of anyone ever pointing out the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ mistakes? No, none whatsoever. Why can’t I be the first?”

“I… I don’t know, but I feel…”

Summoning his courage, Tang Ka said, “I think you’re possessed by extraterritorial devils. That’s why you’re thinking so weirdly.”

“The extraterritorial devils are cunning and capricious. Any of us can be possessed by the extraterritorial devils.”

Chu Zhiyun’s cold face moved, almost as if she were smiling. “If we’re possessed, our mentors will expel the extraterritorial devils for us, and if our mentors are possessed, our director will help them. But think about it. If the Ultimate Benevolence Masters are possessed by the extraterritorial devils without them knowing it, who can help them expel the devils?”

“Hiss—”

Tang Ka almost couldn’t believe that his monitor could be so disobedient. His blood was almost frozen. He said after a long stun, “Blasphemy! That’s the gravest blasphemy!”

“I thought that blasphemy is when you suspect the gods.”

Chu Zhiyun said coldly, “I don’t think the Ultimate Benevolence Masters are gods, are they?”

“Even though they’re not, they’re very close.”

Tang Ka stressed again, “The Ultimate Benevolence Masters are made of the last drop of the gods’ blood in our world. They’re the First Apostles. How can they be corrupted by extraterritorial devils?”

“Even the gods can be corrupted. Wasn’t the tragedy in the primeval war because the Nuwa Clan was corrupted by extraterritorial devils, and all the surviving gods had to hibernate as a result?”

Chu Zhiyun asked back, “The Ultimate Benevolence Masters have only 1% of the power of the gods at most. Why would they be immune?”

“No, no, no. It’s not like that!”

Tang Ka shook his head quickly. “The ending of the primeval war was within the gods’ expectation. They did it on purpose as a test for mankind.”

“Exactly. But how do you know what the gods’ test for me is? Chances are that it’s to point out the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ mistakes.”

Chu Zhiyun took a deep breath and opened her arms. “Have you never been puzzled about our world for one second? Look, everything we know was told to us by someone else. Our training, our missions, and our ideals are all told to us by someone else, and this ‘someone’ is also instructed about what he should do by another someone who is on a higher level.

“Just like that, on the top of the ‘someones’ are exactly the Ultimate Benevolence Masters. It’s said that the Ultimate Benevolence Masters watch over us and guide our way every second. However, getting to the bottom of it, we’ve seen nothing but the illusions of the Ultimate Benevolence Masters!

“My piety to the gods is incomparable, but does believing the gods equal to believing the First Apostles? And does believing the Ultimate Benevolence Masters equal to believing the illusions that call themselves Ultimate Benevolence Masters?

“Tang Ka, look at me in the eyes and think. Hypothetically… I mean, hypothetically, what would you do if the extraterritorial devils had stolen the bodies of the Ultimate Benevolence Masters a long time ago and had been commanding us in the image of the Ultimate Benevolence Masters?”

Tang Ka felt cold deep to the bones.

“That’s a ridiculous and dangerous thought!”

Tang Ka was almost begging Chu Zhiyun. “Please stop thinking, monitor. It’s not a problem that we should consider.”

“Not a problem that we should consider?”

Chu Zhiyun sneered, “Who should consider it then?”

Tang Ka blurted out, “The Ultimate Benevolence Masters.”

The young man realized the absurdity immediately, and Chu Zhiyun said coldly, “You want the Ultimate Benevolence Masters to consider whether or not they’re possessed by extraterritorial devils? Isn’t it like asking someone to leave the ground by pulling their own hair?”

“But…”

Tang Ka finally spoke the truth. “I’m worried about you. I fear that you will be captured and purged in the holy temple when other people learn of your thoughts, and you won’t know yourself or me after that.”

“If the Ultimate Benevolence Masters are the most loyal servants of the gods, they wouldn’t do that,” declared Chu Zhiyun confidently.

“What if they’re not?”

Tang Ka was flabbergasted by his own question, wondering why he had turned so bold too.

“Then, it’s all the more reason why I should step up and point it out for them! It’s my destiny and my duty!” said Chu Zhiyun matter-of-factly.

“Monitor, what do you want exactly? Why do I feel that you want something much more than to be a Lurker?” Tang Ka mumbled.

“Truth. I want all the truth about the gods.”

Chu Zhiyun tried several times and finally put on the most perfect smile.

Chapter 2745: Shouldn’T You Give Us Some Privacy?

Deeply touched by the smile, Tang Ka was dragged to the female clothes area by the girl before he could say anything.

However, he turned around before Chu Zhiyun took off her gray protective suit this time.

He didn’t know why he did that. In the Covenant Alliance, being nude was not embarrassing, like a machine would not be embarrassed to see the internal structure of another machine. As a matter of fact, the people here did not even know what “embarrass” meant.

But for some reason, Tang Ka simply felt that he should… respect her.

Li Yao said in his head, “Huh? Why did you turn around?”

Tang Ka said, “I… I don’t know. What do you mean? What do you want?”

“Nothing. What can I do? I am a man of honor and integrity.. You can rest assured about that. It’s well-known across the universe!”

Li Yao said, “I’m simply interested in the state of your mind and want to study you more deeply!”

“Hey!”

Tang Ka’s messy thoughts were disrupted by Chu Zhiyun’s call.

He looked back, only to see that the girl was not wearing the red dress with gold glitters but a white-and-green dress with a vivid sunflower pattern on it, full of summer vibe.

Tang Ka was captivated, not because of the dress, but because of the smile on her face and the youthfulness behind it.

“I want to listen to the gods’ voice directly, not through the relay of the Ultimate Benevolence Masters. Who dictates that only the Ultimate Benevolence Masters can interpret the will of the gods and that we can’t see and hear the gods directly and know what the gods want us to do?”

Her eyes glowing, Chu Zhiyun said confidently, “If I can’t find an answer in the Covenant Alliance, I’ll search for one in the Imperium. I believe that there must be different theories in that sinful country that illustrate the gods in the opposite way as we do. Then, considering the theories from both ways, I’ll be closer to the truth. That’s why I have to become a Lurker. Tang Ka, is my smile standard? Am I pretty?”

“I… I think so.”

Tang Ka was uneasy, and he did not know what he should look at. He stammered, “Monitor, I think this dress is still too small for you.”

“Oh? Really?”

Chu Zhiyun pulled the hem of the dress and said, “This dress is highly elastic and can automatically fit my curve without hindering me. I’m actually feeling pretty good.”

“But…”

Taking a deep breath, Tang Ka reminded her, “I think you forgot to put on your bra.”

“Oh, you’re right!”

The monitor then moved her arms to her back, trying to unzip the dress.

Tang Ka turned around again, only to feel that the air was thin and that he was passing out.

“Nailed it. It’s your turn now. Come here.”

A moment later, the monitor took a long breath in relief and spoke to Tang Ka.

“Huh?” Stunned again, Tang Ka pointed at his nose. “I’m going to change too?”

“Of course! Why else would I bring you here? Only as a guard?”

Chu Zhiyun picked up a set of male clothes and looked at Tang Ka dangerously. “Mr. Tang Ka, do you want to put them on yourself, or do you want me to do it for you?”

“I… I can do it on my own!”

Under his monitor’s coercion, Tang Ka could only put on the sinful clothes from the Imperium.

He did not know what the clothes were for exactly, probably something between sports and performances. The shirt was an amalgam of broken cloths, with an enormous skull on the chest, and the pants were ragged and full of holes and rivets too.

What the Imperium people considered beautiful was truly creepy!

“What do we do next?”

Tang Ka was uncomfortable wearing these clothes that could tinkle whenever he walked.

Chu Zhiyun smiled, and a piece of light music echoed in the supermarket.

There was no music in the Covenant Alliance, except when everybody gathered and sang after the Ultimate Benevolence Masters to express their admiration for the gods.

Tang Ka had been taught by his mentors that music was one of the evilest things in the universe and the best medium for the extraterrestrial devils to invade one’s head. When people were captured by melodies, extraterrestrial devils would root in their heads.

The mentors had also shown the kids, including Tang Ka, the videos taken in what was called “nightclubs” in the Imperium of True Human Beings. All the people were crazy captives of music and extraterrestrial devils in those videos.

On those evil occasions, fighting, taking in drugs, and public penetrations were not unusual. The reason was simple—music boosted the proliferation of extraterrestrial devils in their heads and completely ruined their brains.

Because of all those videos he watched, Tang Ka was never fond of music. If anything, he was even deeply scared of it.

However, since an extraterrestrial devil named “Grandpa Yao” had already found a way into his head, it seemed unnecessary for him to fear music anymore, right?

Besides, the music was light and delightful, nothing like the videos that his mentors played for them in the past. A golden gate seemed to be opened in front of his eyes, revealing a shining path that led to infinity.

Tang Ka couldn’t help but walk to the source of the music, only to find Chu Zhiyun standing before a peaceful but vigorous illusion.

Inside the 3D light beam was a body of blue water. As the picture zoomed in, green mountains and gold wheat were presented, followed by rising skyscrapers and newly-established towns, as well as the people living in them whose faces were full of happiness.

At the bottom of the light beam were captions—

“Make the first step bravely and change your life today!”

“Choose the Thick Soil Development Group, and choose the best tomorrow!”

“The expedition army of the Imperium will protect your family and your investment. You will be the new master of this new world!”

This was a commercial to attract the investors and colonizers from the rear. There were too many such commercials for the “newly-recovered worlds”.

In the past ten years, although the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance was not over yet, countless citizens of the Imperium thought that the Covenant Alliance had been crushed and that they could make a fortune in the newly-recovered worlds thanks to propaganda.

Therefore, too many colonizers, adventurers, and refugees had come here with their families and their life savings on stinky and crowded immigrant ships.

Now, most of those poor b*stards were stranded in the worlds in the frontline, isolated and panicked under the threat of the Covenant Alliance.

The civilians in the Thick Soil Sector, who took the hit first, must’ve either been killed in the battle or sent to the “holy temples” for purging.

Even so, the beautiful commercial was still extremely appealing and hooked the two ignorant natives of the Covenant Alliance.

At the end of the commercial, a blue ocean and a beach appeared.

In the picture, a tall handsome man and a beautiful woman were hugging and kissing.

Then, a new caption showed up. “The best gift for your love is a new world.”

“There are many similar videos in this place. Many videos actually have plots. They’re called TV series and movies.”

Chu Zhiyun said casually, “Although they’re all make-believe, they’ve captured the daily life and the common behaviors in the Imperium. We can learn to imitate a person of the Imperium through those videos. What do you think?”

“Huh…”

Staring at the gorgeous man and woman who were kissing in the commercial, Tang Ka forgot why he should also learn the imitation.

“They’re ‘kissing’. That’s the name of the action, right? It’s sticking your tongue into the other person’s mouth and licking them.”

Chu Zhiyun said, deep in thought, “It seems to be a common courtesy in the Imperium of True Human Beings. But what’s its point? Can they analyze each other’s genes through the saliva so that they can tell whether or not they can create a stronger and smarter offspring with each other?”

“Well, maybe,” Tang Ka mumbled.

“Don’t say ‘maybe’. We’ll find out after we try it.”

Chu Zhiyun looked at Tang Ka.

“Huh?”

Tang Ka was greatly shocked again.

“Come here.”

Expressionlessly, Chu Zhiyun curled her finger at Tang Ka.

“I… I… Don’t. Wait. You… Wait… Wait!”

Holding his chest, Tang Ka took a deep breath. He felt that the sunflower on his monitor’s dress was as dazzling as the sun, making it impossible for him to think.

However, while his head was blank, he suddenly remembered something else.

“Grandpa Yao.”

Tang Ka focused his attention. “Don’t you think you should give us some privacy?”

“What do you mean?”

Li Yao said, as if he were greatly wronged, “I didn’t say or do anything. You can do whatever you want. I won’t stand in your way. I’ll just stay here and watch in utter silence without causing any disturbance. I’m a decent man!”

Chapter 2746: The Second Swing

“No, I’m feeling very uncomfortable with you being here!”

Tang Ka’s face was red as if it were on force, and an uncanny force pushed Li Yao’s soul out of his brain. “Why don’t you take a rest outside and come back later? The monitor is coming!”

“Wait, where do you want me to take a rest at?”

Li Yao raised his voice and said, “My young friend, let’s make things clear. You’re probably involved in an evil and shameless scheme, and I’m carrying out a mission that concerns the peace of the universe and the future of mankind, which is something very serious. Neither of us should have irrelevant thoughts. Hey, wait, stop pushing me. Don’t be so rejective, or the synchronization rate between our nerves will be affected, and I’m going to be kicked out… friend, kid, little b*stard!”

Despite him shouting, dozens of dazzling electric arcs were generated in Tang Ka’s brain and pushed his soul out determinedly.

Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!

Li Yao lost his senses when he was cast out. He was back to the deep, dark bottom of the ocean, where everything was dark and quiet. He almost couldn’t see or hear anything anymore..

The failsafe system that Xiaoming and Wenwen had bound to his soul was immediately activated. Like a thin, long, and elastic rope, it pulled his soul all the way back through millions of lightyears back to his own shell in the Gold Crystal Pyramid in the Empyreal Terminus Sector.

“Hiss—”

As if he just had the deepest dream, Li Yao sat up from the thick neural interaction fluids. It was not until a long time later that his soul resumed control over his body.

His eyes were focused again, and he felt the feeblest vibrations. He had returned to his world.

“Dad!”

Xiaoming and Wenwen stuck all kinds of sensors to Li Yao and examined his biophysical parameters. “Why were you away for so long? We were worried that your soul might not be able to return! How about it? Did you find a suitable sunspot? Have you found out the plan of the main forces of the Covenant Alliance?”

“I…”

Li Yao picked up a handful of neural interaction fluids. The thick liquid dripped through the gaps between his fingers like threads.

Controlling his own hands felt slightly different from controlling Tang Ka’s.

Spitting out a mouthful of saliva that was mixed with neural interaction fluids, Li Yao gradually remembered what happened in the previous day as his mind turned clearer. He rubbed his temples unhurriedly. “The swing went quite well. I found a very suitable sunspot. Although I haven’t found out the plan of the Covenant Alliance’s army or the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ location, I’m very close, because that young man is involved in some sort of scheme, and theoretically speaking, every scheme requires a mastermind. As soon as the scheme and the mastermind are unraveled, I’m sure I will be able to track down the leadership of the Covenant Alliance.”

“Oh? Really?”

Xiaoming and Wenwen said excitedly, “The sunspot you chose is a young man? How did he react to your arrival? Did he suspect or resist you?”

“Not at all.”

Li Yao shook his head. “The young man trusted and admired me. He begged me to teach him ultimate techniques. However, you know that I can’t keep my soul millions of lightyears away 24 hours a day, and I need to come back to charge myself regularly to deal with unexpected situations.

“When I told him that I would be away for a while, that young man clung to my thigh and would not let me go!”

“Well…”

Xiaoming and Wenwen were suspicious. “Did you have a thigh?”

“My metaphorical thigh!”

Li Yao crawled out of the launch cabin and said, “I’m only saying that I’ve become a mental pillar for the little guy and won his absolute trust. Also, I’ve left some sort of spiritual beacon in his head, and it will be easier for me to land in his head next time.

“I’m so hungry after a night of hard work. Give me some food. Also, what’s the latest reports on the battles on the west and the east fronts? And what’s Long Yangjun’s progress in eliminating the Lurkers in the Imperium? Tell me!”

Although Xiaoming and Wenwen had injected a lot of nutrition into the neural interaction fluids, the energy absorbed through the skin and the veins was far from enough to meet the demand for a long-distance swing.

Therefore, Li Yao had to return to his shell once in a while to rest his brain and replenish himself with tremendous food and crystals.

The battle reports were hardly exciting.

On the east front, the army commanded by Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai secured two consecutive victories, and the remaining fleets of the four families could barely put up any resistance.

However, the four families simply retreated and deployed their troops on the habitable planets, as if they were going to fight the reformists’ army in street battles until all of them died.

Everybody knew that the morale of the four families’ army was not high enough to support street battles, but the reformists’ army did not have the time to fight a war of attrition against the four families either.

Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai’s blades were fast, but now that it had cut into such a stinky mess, the blades could not be drawn out anytime soon.

On the west front, which was the newly-recovered area that Li Yao’s soul just escaped from, the Covenant Alliance had attacked a couple of worlds near the Thick Soil Sector. Limited by their insufficient soldiers, the expedition army of the Imperium could only try to defend without being able to counterattack. It was even safe to say that the Covenant Alliance could take down any Sector if they really wanted it.

After his soul witnessed the catastrophic consequences of the war in person in Black Castle, Li Yao had a deeper understanding of the boring numbers on the battle reports.

Perhaps in the eyes of the leaders like Lei Chenghu or Li Linghai, the situations in the frontline were nothing but figures and charts.

But for Li Yao, the numbers represented the ordinary people who were deceived to go to the frontline for their dreams, only to be captured and brainwashed by the Covenant Alliance, the owners of the colorful but childish palm prints in the empty kindergarten, and also the natives of the Covenant Alliance who were taken away from their parents and who did not have a childhood or a future like Tang Ka.

He had to do something for them!

After chewing infinite Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures as fast as possible until his every cell was filled with explosive power, Li Yao took a deep breath and went into a deep sleep state again.

The super long-distance swing was so exhausting that he didn’t wake up until four hours later.

However, he couldn’t feel more energetic after he woke up. The whole world seemed to have turned clear in his eyes, and his spiritual power was soaring almost like mushrooms after a rain.

“Is this the Sunspot Plan and the Gold Crystal Pyramid’s effects on the training of the soul that Wuying Qi 3.0 mentioned?” Li Yao murmured to himself.

On second thought, it was quite reasonable. Few people in the whole Pangu universe were crazy enough to project their souls to millions of lightyears away for the most dangerous training like he did.

Only craziness could lead to progress.

His way of training was rare even for the top experts!

With that in mind, what was he waiting for?

Li Yao couldn’t wait to get into the head of the little guy named Tang Ka again!

The cocoon launch cabin was opened, and new neural interaction fluids were filled in. All the magic equipment was humming. Xiaoming and Wenwen made an “all set” gesture at Li Yao outside of the transparent cover. Li Yao took a deep breath and buried his head in the thick and warm liquid again.

He flew forward in the deep, dark chaos like a streak of light again.

However, there was a glittering star guiding his way in the infinite darkness this time, like a space gate navigating a starship. It was much less difficult and exhausting than the previous time. Very soon, Li Yao arrived at Tang Ka’s brain.

“Tang Ka, my young friend, are you done?”

Li Yao said delightedly, “I’m back. What a pleasant surprise it must be for you!”

“Huh?”

Tang Ka was stunned for a while and said in complicated feelings, “I… didn’t know that you would come back.”

“What are you talking about? It almost sounds like you don’t want me to return. Am I so unlikeable?”

Li Yao scanned the environment with Tang Ka’s eyes, only to discover that the young man and his monitor had already returned to the rendezvous point.

However, there was obvious tension in the rendezvous point, as Liao Meng was fighting another one of their classmates. The two boys were covered in dust and blood as they wrestled among the collapsed walls.

Chapter 2747: Defectors

“Tell me the truth. What did you do with your monitor while I was away?”

Li Yao asked, “Tell me everything without leaving out any detail!”

“I… I don’t know!”

Tang Ka said, “When we returned, Liao Meng was grappling with this boy named Shao Jie. We’re told that Liao Meng heard Shao Jie whistling a tune of the devils, but Shao Jie denied it, so Liao Meng burst into rage and decided to teach him a lesson!”

“Wait, nobody is asking you why the two boys are fighting!”

Li Yao said, “You didn’t hear my question. I asked you what you did you do with your monitor in the dark corner. Did you do anything else other than the mouth-to-mouth activities?”

“Shao Jie was responsible for identifying the jade chips that contain audios and videos. He might’ve whistled unintentionally when he heard too many of the melodies, but it’s still unusual because we’ve received professional training and shouldn’t have been tempted by such music.”

Tang Ka paused for a moment and said again, “But Liao Meng’s reaction is even more unnatural. He could’ve reported his classmate’s misbehavior to our mentors or just scolded him.. Why did he simply beat the guy up? Liao Meng looks ferocious, but he is never so rash.”

“Hey, hey, hey. Don’t change the subject. Like I said, I’m not asking about the boys!”

Li Yao grew anxious. “I’m asking you and your monitor Chu Zhiyun. What did you do with the girl whom you dream to tie up?”

Tang Ka couldn’t resist anymore. “We didn’t do anything. We simply watched the TV series from the Imperium and learned how to be an emotional and enthusiastic citizen of the Imperium. Why do you insist on asking that?”

“Don’t misunderstand me, kid. As one of the most powerful experts of our universe, I’m not interested in your little private life at all.”

Li Yao coughed and said solemnly, “I’m only trying to remind you that this ultimate test is not so simple and that your every action is likely to be monitored. So, don’t do anything outrageous in any case. But on the other hand, I’m also wondering how to awaken your humanity and turn you into a regular young man!”

“A regular young man, like those in the Imperium?”

Tang Ka snorted and said, “My mentors told us about the lives of the young people in the Imperium before. They plot against each other and live in deception and betrayals every day. Even their closest comrade may turn into the most brutal enemy. At the end of the day, their personal efforts are nothing compared with the influence of their families, and the weak can never change their fate. Grandpa Yao, don’t lie to me. Is it true? Do you want to turn me into such a ‘regular young man’?”

“Of course not!”

Li Yao sensed that the young man’s tongue was sharper and sharper as the ice in his head cracked. He had to think for a moment before he gave a reply.

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said solemnly, “Trust me. I’m not lying to you. There’s a third path in this universe apart from the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance, and there are young men who are nothing like you know!”

“Really?”

The ripples in Tang Ka’s head glittered. “What kind of life do those young people live?”

“In… that place, the young people do not have to blindly worship the groundless gods as you do in the Covenant Alliance or consider everybody else an enemy like those in the Imperium!”

Li Yao declared resolutely, “Those young people can live a more peaceful, happier, and brighter life!

“They’re free to sleep through their classes. They can devote all their time and energy to partying and gaming. They do not worship gods, but they worship the stars that are deified through business approaches and fake commercials. When they burst into rage, they can throw a tantrum at their parents or their teachers. While wasting their lives without the slightest sense of responsibility, they consider themselves the coolest people in the universe. How does it sound? Isn’t it great?”

“Huh…”

Tang Ka said, “You want to turn me into one of those people? Are you sure you’re not an extraterrestrial devil?”

“Well, I thought a young man like you would be interested in those things, but if you’re not, you can also choose to be a good student and contribute to the glorious future of mankind later!”

Li Yao said, “Or maybe you can try to combine the three different paths, making it neither so ‘corrupt’ nor so ‘pious and positive’ as you are right now. All in all, just trust me!”

While the two of them talked silently in Tang Ka’s head, the two bruised young men were separated by their classmates.

Liao Meng was like an angry bull. It took six kids to drag him away, but he was still stomping on the ground, leaving messy footprints there.

“Liao Meng, what are you doing?”

Standing on a collapsed building, Chu Zhiyun looked down at him and said coldly, “It’s the second time that your portable crystal processor has sent out a warning. One more time, and you will be exiled immediately. Nobody will speak for you!”

Her threat was quite effective.

Looking at his portable crystal processor that was glittering in redness, Liao Meng seemed overwhelmed with disbelief, as if he didn’t understand why he lost control of his feelings.

“Second time? It’s already the second time today?”

Having no time to wipe his nosebleed, he gritted his teeth and mumbled.

Actually, not just Liao Meng and Shao Jie, who was corrupted and whistled a tune from the Imperium without him realizing it, but all of the students were teetering on the verge of loss of control.

They had been acting independently so far, or in groups of two like Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka, at most. When twenty students were dispersed in the town, it was like grains of salt being melted in an ocean.

Perhaps they were not purging the town; this filthy, greedy, and evil town was actually tempting and transforming them.

“What do we do?”

Shao Jie, who secretly whistled a tune, could not hide the panic on his face at all. He kept asking Chu Zhiyun, “What do we do?”

Everybody knew that he was not talking about the whistling but the fight with Liao Meng.

There were few internal conflicts within the Covenant Alliance.

According to the Path of Ultimate Benevolence or the Three Fundamental Laws, it was stated that human beings should ensure their own safety and must not hurt each other as long as such protection did not conflict with the First or Second Law.

Although Liao Meng had the power to punish his classmate, the huge fight still bordered on the precipices of a violation.

This could be nothing, but it could be grave too. Even Chu Zhiyun did not know how she should deal with it.

However, it was impossible to cover the two young men’s broken suits and the blood on their clothes. Even if they could cover that, their mentors would still ask about the drastic changes of their biophysical parameters.

Thankfully, an alarm echoed in all directions, saving the two young men’s lives.

Everyone’s mini crystal processors shrieked, giving them a new order on an unexpected, urgent mission!

They were supposed to return to the camp in half an hour, report their actions today, and be examined and blessed by their mentors.

However, two students from another class fled. They seemed to have been corrupted by extraterrestrial devils that were hiding in the myriad of items from the Imperium, and they hurt their monitor and ran to the depths of the city when they were supposed to regather.

It was undoubtedly a violation of the Third Law. It was unpardonable, and they were to be delivered to the holy temple where the sources of their sins would be dug out.

The purge troop stationed next to the city already deployed soldiers to search for the two escaping students. However, the city was too big and complicated, and the number of soldiers was limited. So, the students, including Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka, had to join the hunt too.

At the end of the brief on the urgent mission, the director of the Holy Light Academy solemnly promised that whoever caught the two defectors would receive bonus points, and whoever secretly reported to their mentor on suspicious classmates would also receive bonus points if those classmates were found guilty later.

Such a promise made the hearts of all the Children of Holy Light race.

However, they immediately came back to themselves and tried to look indifferent and heartless by controlling their heartbeats, their breaths, and their micro-expressions, not giving away their real feelings.

“Let’s go and catch those two defectors!”

Chu Zhiyun waved her hand and leaped from the wall. Including Tang Ka, Liao Meng, and Shao Jie, everybody ran in the direction where the two students fled, hoping to capture the two traitors or even blasphemers to cover their uneasiness and demonstrate their piety.

Chapter 2748: Escape At Night

As the night fell, the rain turned heavier.

Perhaps because of the lingering smoke caused by the battle a few days ago, the rainwater was as dark as ink when it fell on the ground, making the place even more fuzzy and chaotic.

The city looked like a rotten corpse, and the unstable skyscrapers everywhere were its bones.

Among the fallen walls, the children rushed under the thunderstorm like hungry hyenas.

Everybody was anxious. They hoped that they would get to capture the two defectors in person.

But they were also slightly panicking, not knowing whether the defection would make them leave a bad impression on the Purgers.

The rain that was as heavy as a waterfall made it impossible to see ten meters away.

The noises of the rain also eclipsed most sounds, including the alarms of their portable crystal processors.

The heavy rain gave the children an unreal sense of safety, making them feel that they could think whatever they wanted without being found.

Their route, on the other hand, had deviated from the safe area that their mentors established, and they entered the unknown areas in the depths of the city.

Some kids lost their way or got tripped over in the ruins, falling behind their partners.

Some kids accidentally noticed each other and confronted each other in the heavy rain, but they left in disappointment after realizing that they were not their targets.

Certain rumors were spreading virally during the brief contact of the kids.

Some said that the two escaping students were a boy and a girl, who were to be purged in the holy temple because of their serious violation when they carried out their mission. That was why they decided to defect and take their chances.

Some said that most areas on Black Castle had been burnt to the ground because of the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance in the past decades, and since the Covenant Alliance was short of soldiers, 99% of the planet was currently unoccupied.

As long as those two students ran to the unoccupied area, they would win. It was impossible for the Covenant Alliance to send a great number of soldiers into the unoccupied area to catch two students.

Some other people said that many secret tunnels and cities, as well as the guerrilla troops of the Imperium, were still hidden below the ground. Those people welcomed the surrender of the young people from the Covenant Alliance. Therefore, those two escaping students were likely going to escape into the underground world and find the guerrilla troops of the Imperium.

As long as they stuck together for a year and a half with the guerrilla troops, if the army of the Imperium came back and reclaimed Black Castle, those two students would successfully immigrate from the Covenant Alliance to the Imperium. Chances were that they would even be described by the Imperium as heroes.

Those rumors burned in the darkness gloomily and could not be distinguished no matter how heavy the storm was.

As for who the “someone” was exactly, none of the kids could tell in their dizziness.

Tang Ka didn’t find the defectors after half a night of searching. He was even separated from his partners after he accidentally fell into a mud pit.

The rain and the mud covered his mask, but after he took off his mask, they were directly flying into his eyes and his mouth, making it impossible for him to keep his eyes open.

Even his portable crystal processor was squeaking. The Spiritual Nexus was also seriously disrupted by the thunderstorm. He could not reach out to his classmate or receive orders from his mentors and his director.

“Monitor!

“Where are you?”

Stumbling and falling, Tang Ka felt that he was a tiny ant and that the magnificent buildings near him were black tombstones. After walking for ten minutes, he couldn’t tell where he was, nor did he see any of his students.

A weird thought suddenly popped up in Tang Ka’s head.

He seemed to be the only person left in this whole town.

His classmates, his mentors, and the soldiers of the purge troop were probably searching for the two escaping students in his opposite direction.

If he were to run at this moment, wouldn’t he have a great chance to escape from this city, enter the unoccupied area, or even find the guerrilla troops of the Imperium?

Didn’t “someone” say that the natives of the Covenant Alliance were welcomed in the Imperium and would be described as “heroes”?

That way, he would be able to completely get rid of his current life.

The flashing thought in his head made Tang Ka tremble so hard that he could not even think that it was his idea.

“Grandpa Yao—” Tang Ka asked, his voice shivering, “Did you direct me to think that way?”

“No, I’m not as unscrupulous as that. It was your own idea.”

After a brief silence, Li Yao continued, “Someone might have guided you to think that way, but it was not me.”

“What… What do you mean?”

Tang Ka was more and more confused.

The world around was like the bloody mouth of a ferocious beast that was swallowing him entirely.

A planet was billions of times more spacious than a starship, but he felt that he was being suffocated.

Calming himself down, Tang Ka soon dropped the absurd thought.

The most important reason was that his monitor was with him. If he were really going to escape, he had to take her with him, right?

No, of course not right! How could he escape? He was faithful to the gods and the Ultimate Benevolence Masters. How could he have such an evil and ridiculous thought? That was the deepest sin!

Huala!

Right then, a bolt of lightning struck and illuminated the street nearby.

Tang Ka saw that someone was moving slowly with his back hunched.

“Hey!”

Tang Ka shouted and went after him, “Here! Which class are you from? Wait for me!”

Perhaps because the rain was too huge, the student did not hear him but still limped forward. He was even faster than before.

However, due to his serious leg injuries, he was soon caught up by Tang Ka.

“Shao Jie?”

Tang Ka turned on a flashlight, only to discover that the student was from his class. He said in excitement, “Why are you here? Where are the other students? Did you see our monitor? Or were you separated from everybody else too?”

“I…”

The boy named Shao Jie stammered, “I got separated from them, and I’m looking for them. Why don’t we split up and search in different directions?”

His face was swollen and deformed because of Liao Meng’s beating, making him look both ugly and pitiful.

Blinking his eyes, Tang Ka moved the flashlight from his face to his back, only to discover that Shao Jie was carrying an enormous backpack and holding two bags. All of them were laden with food—not the synthesized food that the students were given, but the food from the Imperium.

Looking at the guilt in Shao Jie’s eyes, Tang Ka knew everything.

“You want to escape too?”

Tang Ka stepped back and murmured, “How can you betray the Ultimate Benevolence Masters and the gods?”

“I’m not!”

As if he were punched in his face by an invisible fist, Shao Jie screamed, “I’m not running! I’m not!”

But his scream was torn apart by the storm immediately, and he seemed about to be drowned.

“Then why did you pick up such a huge backpack and fill it with the foods that we’re not allowed to eat?”
